<script id="header-5-safe-mic-final-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function bindMic(){
    var b=document.querySelector('#app .header-mainline-v2 .news-mic');
    if(!b)return;
    b.onclick=function(e){
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      if(typeof window.openRunningNewsEditor==='function')window.openRunningNewsEditor();
    };
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',bindMic);else bindMic();
  setTimeout(bindMic,300);setTimeout(bindMic,1000);setInterval(bindMic,2500);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-bangladesh-time-safe-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function bdTime(){
    var d=new Date(),tz='Asia/Dhaka';
    var t=d.toLocaleTimeString('bn-BD',{timeZone:tz,hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',hour12:false});
    var dt=d.toLocaleDateString('bn-BD',{timeZone:tz,weekday:'long',year:'numeric',month:'long',day:'numeric'});
    var ld=d.toLocaleDateString('bn-BD',{timeZone:tz,day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'});
    var a=document.getElementById('appClock'),b=document.getElementById('appDate'),l=document.getElementById('loginDateTime');
    if(a)a.textContent=t;if(b)b.textContent=dt;if(l)l.textContent='সময় '+t+' • '+ld;
  }
  bdTime();setInterval(bdTime,1000);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-4-request-safe-js-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  /* প্রতি সেকেন্ডে একই fixed-width element-এ শুধু text বদলাবে—layout jump হবে না */
  function stableDigitalClock(){
    var el=document.getElementById('appClock'); if(!el)return;
    var d=new Date(), tz='Asia/Dhaka';
    var s=d.toLocaleTimeString('bn-BD',{timeZone:tz,hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit',hour12:false});
    el.textContent=s;
    el.setAttribute('aria-label','বাংলাদেশ সময় '+s);
  }
  stableDigitalClock();
  setInterval(stableDigitalClock,1000);
})();
</script>

<script>
let token='',otpToken='',currentUser=null,media=[],mediaIndex=0,modules=[],drag={};
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const call=(fn,...args)=>new Promise((resolve,reject)=>google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(resolve).withFailureHandler(e=>reject(e))[fn](...args));

window.addEventListener('load',async()=>{initOtp();tick();setInterval(tick,1000);loadPublic();});

function initOtp(){const b=$('otpBoxes');b.innerHTML='';for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const x=document.createElement('input');x.className='otp-box';x.maxLength=1;x.inputMode='numeric';x.addEventListener('input',()=>{x.value=x.value.replace(/\D/g,'');if(x.value&&x.nextElementSibling)x.nextElementSibling.focus()});x.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Backspace'&&!x.value&&x.previousElementSibling)x.previousElementSibling.focus();if(e.key==='Enter')verifyOtp()});b.appendChild(x)}}
function tick(){const d=new Date();const tz='Asia/Dhaka';const time=d.toLocaleTimeString('bn-BD',{timeZone:tz,hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit',hour12:false});const date=d.toLocaleDateString('bn-BD',{timeZone:tz,day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'});const txt='সময় '+time+' • '+date;if($('loginDateTime'))$('loginDateTime').textContent=txt;if($('appClock'))$('appClock').textContent=time;if($('appDate'))$('appDate').textContent=d.toLocaleDateString('bn-BD',{timeZone:tz,weekday:'long',year:'numeric',month:'long',day:'numeric'});}
async function loadPublic(){try{const r=await call('getPublicConfig');fillSignals(r.signals);fillClasses(r.classes)}catch(e){toast('সার্ভার প্রস্তুত হয়নি। Apps Script deploy সেটিং পরীক্ষা করুন।')}}
function fillSignals(list){
  ['loginSignal','appSignal'].forEach(id=>{
    const el=$(id);
    if(!el)return;
    el.innerHTML='<option value="">সংকেত — বিষয় নির্বাচন করুন</option>';
    (list||[]).forEach(s=>{
      const o=document.createElement('option');
      o.value=s[0];
      o.textContent=s[0]+' — '+(s[1]||'বিষয়');
      o.dataset.label=s[1]||'';
      el.appendChild(o);
    });
  });
}
function syncSearchSignal(where){const sig=$(where==='login'?'loginSignal':'appSignal').value;const input=$(where==='login'?'loginSearch':'appSearch');if(sig){input.value='';input.placeholder=sig+' — এখানে নিজস্ব ID / মোবাইল / ইমেইল লিখুন';input.focus()}else{input.value='';input.placeholder='সংকেত নির্বাচন করে ID / মোবাইল / ইমেইল লিখুন'}}
function fillClasses(c){/* class list is available through module forms */}
function togglePassword(){const x=$('password');x.type=x.type==='password'?'text':'password'}
async function login(){
  const u=$('username')?.value.trim()||'', p=$('password')?.value||'';
  const btn=$('mainLoginBtn');
  if(!u||!p){toast('ইউজার নেম ও পাসওয়ার্ড দিন।');return;}
  if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.dataset.oldText=btn.textContent;btn.textContent='⏳ লগইন হচ্ছে...';}
  try{
    if(typeof google==='undefined'||!google.script||!google.script.run)throw new Error('Apps Script সংযোগ পাওয়া যায়নি। এই HTML ফাইলটি Apps Script Web App হিসেবে Deploy করুন।');
    const r=await call('login',u,p);
    if(!r||!r.ok){toast((r&&r.message)||'লগইন ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।');return;}
    if(!r.requiresOtp){
      token=r.token;currentUser=r.user||{};
      $('otpArea').classList.remove('show');
      $('loginPage').style.display='none';
      $('app').classList.add('show');
      await loadApp();
      return;
    }
    otpToken=r.token;
    $('otpArea').classList.add('show');
    $('otpMsg').textContent=(r.message||'OTP পাঠানো হয়েছে।')+' '+(r.emailMask||'');
    const first=$('otpBoxes')?.firstElementChild;if(first)first.focus();
  }catch(e){
    toast('লগইন সার্ভার ত্রুটি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e));
  }finally{
    if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent=btn.dataset.oldText||'লগইন';}
  }
} 
async function verifyOtp(){const code=[...document.querySelectorAll('.otp-box')].map(x=>x.value).join('');if(code.length!==6)return toast('৬ সংখ্যার OTP দিন।');const r=await call('verifyLoginOtp',otpToken,$('username').value.trim(),code);if(!r.ok)return toast(r.message);token=r.token;currentUser=r.user;$('loginPage').style.display='none';$('app').classList.add('show');await loadApp()}
async function resendOtp(){const r=await call('requestOtp',$('username').value.trim());toast(r.message)}
function clearOtp(){document.querySelectorAll('.otp-box').forEach(x=>x.value='');$('otpBoxes').firstElementChild.focus()}
async function resetOtp(){const r=await call('requestOtp',$('resetUser').value.trim());toast(r.message)}
async function logout(){await call('logout',token);location.reload()}
async function loadApp(){const r=await call('getBootstrap',token);if(!r||!r.ok)return;var ub=currentUser||r.currentAdmin||{};var rr=String(ub.Role||ub.role||'').toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g,'_');var isSuper=(rr==='SUPER_ADMIN'||rr==='SUPERADMIN');if($('accountManagerBtn'))$('accountManagerBtn').style.display=isSuper?'inline-block':'none';if($('sheetWin'))$('sheetWin').dataset.allowed=isSuper?'yes':'no';var sheetBtn=document.querySelector('.sheet-btn');if(sheetBtn)sheetBtn.style.display=isSuper?'inline-block':'none';modules=r.modules||[];renderModules();renderStats(r.stats||{});renderNotices(r.notices||[]);media=r.media||[];renderMedia();fillFeatureSelect();if(isSuper)fillSheets();else if($('sheetSelect'))$('sheetSelect').innerHTML='';dragWindow($('formWin'))}
function renderModules(){const l=$('leftModules'),r=$('rightModules');l.innerHTML='';r.innerHTML='';modules.forEach((m,i)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='module';b.textContent=m[1];b.onclick=()=>openModule(m[0]);(i<Math.ceil(modules.length/2)?l:r).appendChild(b)})}
function fillFeatureSelect(){const s=$('featureSelect');modules.forEach(m=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=m[0];o.textContent=m[1];s.appendChild(o)})}
function renderStats(x){
  const arr=[['আয়',x.income],['ব্যয়',x.expense],['মোট ক্যাশ',x.cash],['মোট বকেয়া',x.due],['মোট ছাত্র/ছাত্রী',x.students],['মোট শিক্ষক/শিক্ষিকা',x.teachers],['নির্বাহী পরিষদ সদস্য',x.executives],['নিবন্ধিত মহিলা মাদ্রাসা',x.femaleMadrasa],['নিবন্ধিত পুরুষ মাদ্রাসা',x.maleMadrasa],['আজ উপস্থিত',x.attendanceToday.present],['আজ অনুপস্থিত',x.attendanceToday.absent]];
  $('summary').innerHTML=arr.map(a=>`<div class="stat"><b>${a[1]??0}</b>${a[0]}</div>`).join('');
var sum=$('summary'); if(sum) sum.classList.add('dashboard-slider-summary');
var attendanceBox=[...sum.querySelectorAll('.stat')].find(function(el){return el.textContent.indexOf('আজ উপস্থিত')>=0;});
if(attendanceBox){
  var exam=document.createElement('div');exam.className='stat';exam.innerHTML='<b>0</b>পরীক্ষার্থী';attendanceBox.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',exam);
  var adminBox=document.createElement('div');adminBox.className='stat';adminBox.innerHTML='<b>'+((x.activeAdmins!=null)?x.activeAdmins:0)+'</b>মোট একটিভ/অনলাইন অ্যাডমিন সংখ্যা';exam.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',adminBox);
  var helpBox=document.createElement('div');helpBox.className='stat quick-help-stat';helpBox.innerHTML='<button class="quick-help-dashboard-btn" onclick="openWindow(\'quickWin\')">🆘 দ্রুত সেবা / হেল্প</button>';attendanceBox.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',helpBox);
}

  renderDashboardCharts(x);
}
function renderDashboardCharts(x){
  const income=Number(x.income)||0, expense=Number(x.expense)||0, cash=Number(x.cash)||0, due=Number(x.due)||0;
  const max=Math.max(income,expense,cash,due,1);
  const pct=v=>Math.max(4,Math.round(v/max*100));
  const p=Number(x.attendanceToday.present)||0, a=Number(x.attendanceToday.absent)||0, total=p+a, pp=total?Math.round(p/total*100):0;
  const periods=['দৈনিক','সাপ্তাহিক','মাসিক','বাৎসরিক'];
  $('dashboardCharts').innerHTML=`
    <div class="chart-card">
      <div class="chart-title">📊 আয় + ব্যয় + ক্যাশ + বকেয়া — সময়ভিত্তিক সামারি</div>
      <div class="period-tabs">${periods.map((v,i)=>`<button class="period-tab ${i===0?'active':''}" onclick="selectDashboardPeriod(this,'${v}')">${v}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="bar-chart">
        ${[['আয়',income,'income'],['ব্যয়',expense,'expense'],['ক্যাশ',cash,'cash'],['বকেয়া',due,'expense']].map(v=>`<div class="bar-group"><div class="bar-pair"><div class="bar ${v[2]}" style="height:${pct(v[1])}%" title="${v[1]}"></div></div><div class="bar-label">${v[0]}</div></div>`).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="legend"><span><i class="dot" style="background:#20a464"></i>আয়</span><span><i class="dot" style="background:#e33b4a"></i>ব্যয়/বকেয়া</span><span><i class="dot" style="background:#248bd0"></i>ক্যাশ</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="chart-card">
      <div class="chart-title">📈 দৈনিক উপস্থিতি / অনুপস্থিতি</div>
      <div class="attendance-box">
        <div class="attendance-ring" style="--present:${pp}%"><span>${pp}%</span></div>
        <div class="attendance-counts"><div class="attendance-count"><b>${p}</b>উপস্থিত</div><div class="attendance-count"><b>${a}</b>অনুপস্থিত</div></div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
}
function selectDashboardPeriod(btn,period){document.querySelectorAll('.period-tab').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));btn.classList.add('active');toast(period+' সামারি নির্বাচন করা হয়েছে');}

function renderNotices(n){const box=$('noticeArea');if(!box)return;box.innerHTML='<h4>সাম্প্রতিক নোটিশ</h4>'+(n||[]).map(x=>`<div class="card" style="padding:8px;margin:5px 0"><b>${x.TitleBN||''}</b><div>${x.BodyBN||''}</div></div>`).join('')}
function renderMedia(){
  const box=$('mediaStageContent');if(!box)return;if(!media.length){box.innerHTML='<span>মিডিয়া আপলোড করে স্লাইড দেখুন</span>';return}
  const x=media[mediaIndex%media.length], u=String(x.DataURL||'');
  if(x.Type==='image')box.innerHTML='<img src="'+u+'" alt="'+(x.Name||'')+'">';
  else if(x.Type==='video')box.innerHTML='<video src="'+u+'" controls preload="metadata"></video>';
  else box.innerHTML='<audio src="'+u+'" controls preload="metadata"></audio>';
  dashboardMediaPlaying=false;const b=$('mediaPlayBtn');if(b)b.textContent='▶ Play';
}
function nextMedia(){if(media.length){mediaIndex=(mediaIndex+1)%media.length;renderMedia()}}
function prevMedia(){if(media.length){mediaIndex=(mediaIndex-1+media.length)%media.length;renderMedia()}}
async function deleteCurrentMedia(){if(!media.length)return;const current=media[mediaIndex%media.length],r=await call('deleteMedia',token,current.Serial);toast(r.ok?'মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে':'ব্যর্থ');if(r.ok){media.splice(mediaIndex,1);mediaIndex=media.length?mediaIndex%media.length:0;renderMedia();}}
function openModule(key){
const labels=Object.fromEntries(modules.map(x=>[x[0],x[1]]));
$('winTitle').textContent=labels[key]||key;
$('winBody').innerHTML='<div class="form-language-fixed"><span>বাংলা</span><span>العربية</span><span>English</span></div>'+moduleForm(key)+'<div class="form-signatures"><div class="signature-box"><div>আবেদনকারীর স্বাক্ষর</div><div class="signature-line"></div></div><div class="signature-box"><div>অভিভাবকের স্বাক্ষর</div><div class="signature-line"></div></div><div class="signature-box"><div>প্রধান শিক্ষিকার স্বাক্ষর</div><div class="signature-line"></div></div><div class="signature-box"><div>প্রিন্সিপাল/মুহতামিম এর স্বাক্ষর</div><div class="signature-line"></div></div></div><div class="form-action-bar"><button type="button" class="primary" onclick="saveCurrentForm()">💾 সেভ</button><button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="printCurrentForm()">🖨️ প্রিন্ট</button><button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="enableFormEdit()">✏️ এডিট</button><button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="downloadCurrentForm()">⬇️ ডাউনলোড</button><button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="clearFormWindow()">🧹 ক্লিয়ার</button></div>';
$('formWin').classList.remove('minimized');
$('formWin').style.height='';
openWindow('formWin');
setTimeout(function(){clearFormWindow(false);},0);
}
function locationField(id,label,options){
  return `<label>${label} / Dropdown<select id="${id}Select" class="field"><option value="">নির্বাচন করুন</option>${options.map(x=>`<option>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select><input id="${id}Manual" class="field" placeholder="হাতে লিখুন"></label>`;
}

async function saveModule(k){
const map={student:'STUDENTS',teacher:'TEACHERS',donor:'DONORS'};const s=map[k];
const d={NameBN:$('fNameBN').value,NameAR:$('fNameAR').value,NameEN:$('fNameEN').value,
FatherNameBN:$('fFatherBN').value,FatherNameAR:$('fFatherAR').value,FatherNameEN:$('fFatherEN').value,
MotherNameBN:$('fMotherBN').value,MotherNameAR:$('fMotherAR').value,MotherNameEN:$('fMotherEN').value,
Mobile:$('fMobile').value,WhatsApp:$('fWhatsApp').value,Email:$('fEmail').value,Address:$('fAddress').value,
Division:getLocationValue('fDivision'),District:getLocationValue('fDistrict'),Upazila:getLocationValue('fUpazila'),Union:getLocationValue('fUnion'),Ward:getLocationValue('fWard'),Village:getLocationValue('fVillage'),
Class:document.getElementById('fClass')?.value||'',Department:document.getElementById('fDept')?.value||'',Branch:document.getElementById('fBranch')?.value||'',Photo:await fileData('fPhoto'),BirthReg:document.getElementById('fBirth')?.value||'',NID:document.getElementById('fNid')?.value||''};
if(k==='student')d.Gender='';const r=await call('saveRecord',token,s,d);toast(r.ok?'সফলভাবে সংরক্ষণ হয়েছে':'ব্যর্থ')}
async function saveAdmin(){const photo=await fileData('aPhoto');const d={NameBN:$('aName').value,NameAR:$('aAr').value,NameEN:$('aEn').value,Username:$('aUser').value,Password:$('aPass').value,Email:$('aEmail').value,Mobile:$('aMobile').value,WhatsApp:$('aWa').value,ApprovalID:$('aApproval').value,Address:$('aAddress').value,Photo:photo,BirthReg:$('aBirth').value,NID:$('aNid').value};const r=await call('createAdmin',token,d);toast(r.message||('Admin ID: '+r.adminId))}
async function savePayment(){const d={StudentID:$('pStudent').value,Name:$('pName').value,Month:$('pMonth').value,Category:$('pCat').value,Amount:Number($('pAmount').value),Method:$('pMethod').value,Date:new Date().toISOString(),Note:$('pNote').value};const r=await call('saveRecord',token,'PAYMENTS',d);toast(r.ok?'Payment saved':'Failed');}
async function saveResult(k){const d={StudentID:$('rStudent').value,Name:$('rName').value,Class:$('rClass').value,Exam:$('rExam').value,SubjectData:$('rSubjects').value,Published:'NO',Date:new Date().toISOString()};const r=await call('saveRecord',token,'RESULTS',d);toast(r.ok?'Saved':'Failed')}
async function uploadMedia(){
  const input=$('mediaFiles');
  if(!input||!input.files||!input.files.length)return toast('প্রথমে ফটো/ভিডিও/অডিও নির্বাচন করুন।');
  let uploaded=0,failed=0;
  const selected=[...input.files];
  const selectedCount={image:0,video:0,audio:0};
  selected.forEach(function(f){
    const t=f.type.startsWith('image/')?'image':f.type.startsWith('video/')?'video':f.type.startsWith('audio/')?'audio':'';
    if(t)selectedCount[t]++;
  });
  if(selectedCount.image>100)return toast('একসাথে সর্বোচ্চ 100টি ফটো নির্বাচন করা যাবে।');
  if(selectedCount.video>20)return toast('একসাথে সর্বোচ্চ 20টি ভিডিও নির্বাচন করা যাবে।');
  if(selectedCount.audio>50)return toast('একসাথে সর্বোচ্চ 50টি অডিও নির্বাচন করা যাবে।');
  try{
    for(const f of selected){
      const type=f.type.startsWith('image/')?'image':f.type.startsWith('video/')?'video':f.type.startsWith('audio/')?'audio':'';
      if(!type){failed++;toast(f.name+' অনুমোদিত নয়।');continue}
      const max=type==='image'?20:50;
      if(f.size>max*1024*1024){failed++;toast(f.name+' — সর্বোচ্চ '+max+' MB।');continue}
      toast('আপলোড হচ্ছে: '+f.name+' — 0%');
      const dataUrl=await new Promise(function(resolve,reject){
        const fr=new FileReader();
        fr.onload=function(){resolve(fr.result||'');};
        fr.onerror=function(){reject(new Error('ফাইল পড়া যায়নি।'));};
        fr.readAsDataURL(f);
      });
      const r=await call('saveMedia',token,{name:f.name,type:type,size:f.size,mimeType:f.type,dataUrl:dataUrl,sort:0});
      if(r&&r.ok){uploaded++;toast('আপলোড 100% সম্পন্ন: '+f.name);}
      else{failed++;toast((r&&r.message)||('আপলোড ব্যর্থ: '+f.name));}
    }
    media=await call('listMedia',token);media=Array.isArray(media)?media:[];
    mediaIndex=media.length?Math.min(mediaIndex,media.length-1):0;
    renderMedia();if(typeof renderMediaList==='function')renderMediaList();
    if(uploaded&&failed)toast(uploaded+'টি আপলোড হয়েছে, '+failed+'টি ব্যর্থ।');
    else if(uploaded)toast(uploaded+'টি মিডিয়া সফলভাবে আপলোড হয়েছে।');
    else toast('কোনো মিডিয়া আপলোড হয়নি।');
    input.value='';
  }catch(e){toast('আপলোডে সার্ভার ত্রুটি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:String(e)))}
}
function renderMediaList(){const box=$('mediaList');if(!box)return;box.innerHTML=media.map(function(x,i){return '<div class="media-list-item"><span>'+escapeHtml(x.Name||('Media '+(i+1)))+'</span><button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="deleteMediaByIndex('+i+')">🗑 Delete</button></div>';}).join('');}
async function deleteMediaByIndex(i){if(!media[i])return;const r=await call('deleteMedia',token,media[i].Serial);if(r&&r.ok){media.splice(i,1);if(mediaIndex>=media.length)mediaIndex=Math.max(0,media.length-1);renderMedia();renderMediaList();toast('মিডিয়া মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে।')}else toast((r&&r.message)||'ডিলিট ব্যর্থ')}
async function fileData(id){const f=$(id)?.files?.[0];return f?await readFile(f):''}function readFile(f){return new Promise((res,rej)=>{const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>res(r.result);r.onerror=rej;r.readAsDataURL(f)})}
async function doSearch(where){const sig=$(where==='login'?'loginSignal':'appSignal').value,q=$(where==='login'?'loginSearch':'appSearch').value.trim();if(!sig)return toast('প্রথমে সংকেত নির্বাচন করুন।');if(!q)return toast('ID / মোবাইল / ইমেইল দিন।');const r=await call('searchAll',token,sig,q);if(!r.ok)return toast(r.message);$('searchBody').innerHTML=`<h3>${r.signal} / ${r.query} — ${r.count} ফলাফল</h3>`+table(r.matches);openWindow('searchWin')}
function table(rows){if(!rows.length)return '<p>কোন তথ্য পাওয়া যায়নি।</p>';const h=Object.keys(rows[0]);return '<div class="table-wrap"><table class="data-table"><thead><tr>'+h.map(x=>`<th>${x}</th>`).join('')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.map(r=>'<tr>'+h.map(x=>`<td>${String(r[x]??'')}</td>`).join('')+'</tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'}
async function loadSheet(){var u=window.currentUser||{};var role=String(u.Role||u.role||'').toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g,'_');if(role!=='SUPER_ADMIN'&&role!=='SUPERADMIN'){closeWindow('sheetWin');return toast('শুধু Super Admin মূল শিটে প্রবেশ করতে পারবেন।')}const s=$('sheetSelect').value;if(!s)return;const r=await call('getSheetData',token,s,300);if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.message)||'শিট ডাটা পাওয়া যায়নি।');$('sheetBody').innerHTML=table(r.rows)}
async function fillSheets(){const r=await call('getPublicConfig');$('sheetSelect').innerHTML=['ADMINS','STUDENTS','TEACHERS','DONORS','PAYMENTS','EXPENSES','RESULTS','ATTENDANCE','NOTICES','MEDIA','FILES','MADRASAS'].map(s=>`<option>${s}</option>`).join('')}
async function sendQuick(){
  const data={name:$('qName').value.trim(),mobile:$('qMobile').value.trim(),email:$('qEmail').value.trim(),message:$('qMsg').value.trim()};
  if(!data.message)return toast('মেসেজ লিখুন।');
  try{const r=await call('sendQuickService',token,data);toast(r.message);if(r.ok)closeWindow('quickWin');}catch(e){toast('মেসেজ পাঠাতে সার্ভার ত্রুটি হয়েছে।');}
}
function normalizeQuickPhone(v){
  var x=String(v||'').replace(/[^0-9+]/g,'');
  if(x.indexOf('+880')===0)return '880'+x.slice(4);
  if(x.indexOf('880')===0)return x;
  if(x.indexOf('0')===0)return '880'+x.slice(1);
  return x;
}
function syncQuickContact(){
  var v=$('qMobile')&&$('qMobile').value.trim();
  var n=normalizeQuickPhone(v);
  var valid=/^8801[3-9][0-9]{8}$/.test(n);
  var wb=$('quickWaBtn'),cb=$('quickCallBtn');
  if(wb)wb.disabled=!valid;if(cb)cb.disabled=!valid;
}
function openQuickWhatsApp(){
  var n=normalizeQuickPhone($('qMobile').value);if(!/^8801[3-9][0-9]{8}$/.test(n))return toast('সঠিক WhatsApp মোবাইল নম্বর দিন।');
  var msg=$('qMsg').value.trim()||'জান্নাতুল বাক্বী ডিজিটাল প্লাটফর্ম থেকে যোগাযোগ';
  window.open('https://wa.me/'+n+'?text='+encodeURIComponent(msg),'_blank');
}
function callQuickMobile(){
  var v=$('qMobile').value.trim();if(!v)return toast('মোবাইল নম্বর দিন।');
  window.location.href='tel:'+v;
}

function openWindow(id){$(id).classList.add('show')}
function closeWindow(id){
  $(id).classList.remove('show');
  if(id==='formWin'){
    $('formWin').classList.remove('minimized');
    $('formWin').style.height='';
  }
}
function minimizeWin(){
  var w=$('formWin');
  w.classList.toggle('minimized');
  w.style.height=w.classList.contains('minimized')?'48px':'';
}
function clearFormWindow(showToast){
  var root=$('winBody');if(!root)return;
  if(showToast!==false){
    var ok=window.confirm('⚠️ সতর্কতা!\n\nফর্মের বর্তমান সব তথ্য মুছে যাবে।\nআপনি কি নিশ্চিতভাবে ক্লিয়ার করতে চান?');
    if(!ok){toast('ক্লিয়ার বাতিল করা হয়েছে');return;}
  }
  root.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,select').forEach(function(el){
    if(el.type==='file'){try{el.value='';}catch(e){}}
    else if(el.tagName==='SELECT'){el.selectedIndex=0;}
    else if(el.type!=='button' && el.type!=='submit'){el.value='';}
  });
  if(showToast!==false)toast('ফর্মের তথ্য পরিষ্কার করা হয়েছে');
}
function saveCurrentForm(){
  var t=$('winTitle').textContent||'';
  var m=modules.find(function(x){return x[1]===t});
  if(m && typeof saveModule==='function' && ['student','teacher','donor'].includes(m[0])) return saveModule(m[0]);
  if(m && m[0]==='admin' && typeof saveAdmin==='function') return saveAdmin();
  toast('ফর্ম প্রস্তুত আছে — সংশ্লিষ্ট Save অপশন ব্যবহার করুন।');
}
function enableFormEdit(){
  var root=$('winBody');if(!root)return;
  root.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,select').forEach(function(el){el.disabled=false;el.readOnly=false;});
  toast('✏️ এডিট মোড চালু হয়েছে');
}
function printCurrentForm(){
  var w=$('formWin');if(!w)return;
  w.classList.remove('minimized');
  window.print();
}
function downloadCurrentForm(){
  var title=($('winTitle').textContent||'form').replace(/[^\\w\\u0980-\\u09FF\\u0600-\\u06FF-]+/g,'_');
  var body=$('winBody').cloneNode(true);
  body.querySelectorAll('.form-action-bar').forEach(function(x){x.remove();});
  body.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,select').forEach(function(el){
    var v=el.value||'';
    var span=document.createElement('span');
    span.textContent=v;
    span.style.display='inline-block';
    span.style.minWidth='120px';
    span.style.borderBottom='1px solid #777';
    span.style.padding='3px';
    el.replaceWith(span);
  });
  var html='<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>'+title+'</title></head><body><h2>'+($('winTitle').textContent||'ফর্ম')+'</h2>'+body.innerHTML+'</body></html>';
  var blob=new Blob([html],{type:'text/html;charset=utf-8'});
  var url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;a.download=title+'.html';document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();
  setTimeout(function(){URL.revokeObjectURL(url);},1000);
  toast('ফর্ম ডাউনলোড করা হয়েছে');
}
function goHome(){window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'})}
function setMode(m){document.body.classList.remove('dark','gold');if(m==='dark')document.body.classList.add('dark');if(m==='gold')document.body.classList.add('gold');localStorage.mode=m}
function setLang(l){localStorage.lang=l;document.documentElement.dir=l==='ar'?'rtl':'ltr'}
function toast(t){const x=$('toast');x.textContent=t;x.style.display='block';clearTimeout(window._toast);window._toast=setTimeout(()=>x.style.display='none',3500)}
function dragWindow(el){
  const head=el.querySelector('.win-head');
  if(!head)return;
  head.onmousedown=function(e){
    if(e.target.closest('button'))return;
    const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),sx=e.clientX,sy=e.clientY;
    el.style.transform='none';
    el.style.left=r.left+'px';el.style.top=r.top+'px';
    function mv(ev){
      let x=r.left+ev.clientX-sx, y=r.top+ev.clientY-sy;
      const maxX=Math.max(0,window.innerWidth-el.offsetWidth), maxY=Math.max(0,window.innerHeight-8);
      x=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxX,x)); y=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxY,y));
      el.style.left=x+'px';el.style.top=y+'px';
    }
    function up(){document.removeEventListener('mousemove',mv);document.removeEventListener('mouseup',up);}
    document.addEventListener('mousemove',mv);document.addEventListener('mouseup',up);
  };
}
</script>

})();

      w.style.transition='opacity .5s ease';
      w.style.opacity='0';
      setTimeout(function(){if(w)w.remove();},500);
    },5000);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',startWelcome);
  else startWelcome();
})();
</script>

<script id="header-final-arrange">
(function(){
  function arrange(){
    const h=document.querySelector('header')||document.querySelector('.header')||document.querySelector('.top-header');
    if(!h)return;
    // Make the running ticker a single line.
    h.querySelectorAll('.ticker,[class*=ticker],marquee').forEach(e=>{e.style.whiteSpace='nowrap';e.style.overflow='hidden';e.style.width='100%';});
    // Do not alter existing event handlers; only normalize visible row wrapping.
    h.querySelectorAll('.row,.header-row').forEach(e=>{e.style.flexWrap='nowrap';});
  }
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',arrange);
  setTimeout(arrange,300);
})();
</script>

<script id="dashboard-two-small-boxes-inside-slider-js">
(function(){
  function placeChartsInsideSlider(){
    var slider=document.querySelector('.dashboard-slider');
    var charts=document.getElementById('dashboardCharts');
    var summary=document.getElementById('summary');
    if(slider && charts && charts.parentElement!==slider){
      if(summary && summary.parentElement===slider) summary.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',charts);
      else slider.appendChild(charts);
    }
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',placeChartsInsideSlider);
  else placeChartsInsideSlider();
  setTimeout(placeChartsInsideSlider,300);
  setTimeout(placeChartsInsideSlider,800);
})();
</script>

<script id="search-profile-final-js">
(function(){
  function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]})}
  function first(r,keys){for(var i=0;i<keys.length;i++){if(r&&r[keys[i]]!==undefined&&r[keys[i]]!==null&&String(r[keys[i]])!=='')return r[keys[i]]}return ''}
  function isStudent(r){var s=JSON.stringify(r||{}).toLowerCase();return /student|studentid|ছাত্র|ছাত্রী/.test(s)||first(r,['Class','শ্রেনি/জামাত','শ্রেণি','NameBN'])!==''}
  function qrUrl(text){return 'https://quickchart.io/qr?size=140&text='+encodeURIComponent(text||'Jannatul Baqi Digital Platform 2026')}
  function logoSrc(){var x=document.getElementById('headerLogo')||document.getElementById('loginLogo');return x&&x.src?x.src:''}
  window.renderSearchProfiles=function(rows,signal,q){
    var logo=logoSrc();
    var head='<div class="search-profile-head"><img class="sp-logo" src="'+esc(logo)+'" alt="Logo"><div class="sp-inst"><div class="bn">জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানা</div><div class="ar">المدرسة الجنة الباقية للبنات ودار الايتام</div><div class="en">Jannatul Baky Mohila Madrasha &amp; Eatimkhana</div><div class="sp-contact">উপজেলা মডেল টাউন কোনাখোলা, কেরাণীগঞ্জ, ঢাকা-১৩১০<br>মোবাইল: 01823316630, 01717339288<br>ইমেইল: hmsumon789@gmail.com, jannatubakymrs@gmail.com</div></div><img class="sp-qr" src="'+qrUrl(signal+' '+q)+'" alt="QR Code"></div>';
    var cards=(rows||[]).map(function(r,idx){
      var photo=first(r,['Photo','ছবি','PhotoURL','Image','ImageURL']);
      /* Search Profile-এ Photo কলামের URL/ID কখনো লেখা হিসেবে দেখাবে না—শুধু ছবি। */
      if(photo){
        var ps=String(photo).trim(), gm=ps.match(/(?:id=|\/d\/|file\/d\/|open\?id=)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,})/i);
        if(/^data:image\//i.test(ps)){photo=ps;}
        else if(/^DRIVE:/i.test(ps)){photo='https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(ps.slice(6))+'&sz=w800';}
        else if(gm){photo='https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(gm[1])+'&sz=w800';}
        else if(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}$/.test(ps)){photo='https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(ps)+'&sz=w800';}
        else if(/^[A-Za-z0-9+/=]{1000,}$/.test(ps)){photo='data:image/jpeg;base64,'+ps;}
        else {photo='';}
      }
      var title=first(r,['NameBN','নাম','Name','NameEN'])||'স্টুডেন্ট তথ্য';
      var fields=[['স্টুডেন্ট ID',first(r,['StudentID','ID','Serial','Student Id'])],['নাম',first(r,['NameBN','Name','নাম'])],['আরবি নাম',first(r,['NameAR'])],['English Name',first(r,['NameEN'])],['মোবাইল',first(r,['Mobile','মোবাইল'])],['WhatsApp',first(r,['WhatsApp'])],['ইমেইল',first(r,['Email','ইমেইল'])],['শ্রেণি',first(r,['Class','শ্রেনি','শ্রেণি/জামাত'])],['বিভাগ',first(r,['Department','বিভাগ'])],['শাখা',first(r,['Branch','শাখা'])],['ঠিকানা',first(r,['Address','ঠিকানা'])],['জন্ম নিবন্ধন',first(r,['BirthReg','জন্ম নিবন্ধন'])],['NID',first(r,['NID'])]];
      var img=photo?'<img class="sp-photo" src="'+esc(photo)+'" alt="Student Photo">':'<div class="sp-photo-empty">ছবি নেই</div>';
      return '<div class="sp-student"><div class="sp-student-top"><div class="sp-student-info"><div class="sp-student-title">'+esc(title)+' — স্টুডেন্ট তথ্য</div><div class="sp-student-note">স্টুডেন্ট তথ্য</div></div><div class="sp-photo-wrap">'+img+'<div class="sp-photo-label">স্টুডেন্ট ফটো</div></div></div><div class="sp-data">'+fields.map(function(f){return '<div class="sp-field"><b>'+esc(f[0])+'</b><span>'+esc(f[1])+'</span></div>'}).join('')+'</div></div>';
    }).join('');
    var actions='<div class="sp-bottom-actions"><button class="primary" onclick="saveSearchProfile()">💾 সেভ</button><button class="secondary" onclick="editSearchProfile()">✏️ এডিট</button><button class="secondary" onclick="printSearchProfile()">🖨️ প্রিন্ট</button><button class="secondary" onclick="downloadSearchProfile()">⬇️ ডাউনলোড</button></div>';
    $('searchBody').innerHTML='<div id="searchProfilePrint" class="search-profile">'+head+'<div class="sp-search-meta"><b>সার্চ: '+esc(signal)+' / '+esc(q)+' — '+(rows||[]).length+' ফলাফল</b></div>'+cards+actions+'</div>';
  };
  window.printSearchProfile=function(){var x=document.getElementById('searchProfilePrint');if(!x)return;var w=window.open('','_blank','width=1000,height=900');w.document.write('<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>সার্চ প্রোফাইল</title><style>@page{size:A4 portrait;margin:10mm}body{font-family:Arial,"Noto Sans Bengali",sans-serif;margin:0;color:#111}.search-profile{width:190mm;min-height:277mm;box-sizing:border-box;padding:0}.search-profile-head{display:grid;grid-template-columns:82px 1fr 82px;gap:10px;align-items:center;border-bottom:2px solid #111;padding-bottom:8px}.sp-logo,.sp-qr{width:70px;height:70px;object-fit:contain}.sp-inst{text-align:center;font-weight:900;line-height:1.3}.sp-inst .bn{font-size:20px}.sp-inst .ar{font-size:17px}.sp-inst .en{font-size:15px}.sp-contact{font-size:11px;font-weight:800}.sp-student{border:2px solid #111;margin-top:8px;padding:8px;break-inside:avoid}.sp-student-top{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 120px;gap:10px;min-height:120px;align-items:start}.sp-student-info{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:flex-start;min-height:100px}.sp-student-title{font-size:19px;font-weight:900}.sp-student-note{display:none}.sp-photo-wrap{text-align:center}.sp-photo,.sp-photo-empty{width:105px;height:125px;object-fit:cover;border:2px solid #111}.sp-photo-empty{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;text-align:center}.sp-photo-label{font-size:11px;font-weight:900;margin-top:2px}.sp-data{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:5px;margin-top:8px}.sp-field{border:1px solid #555;padding:5px;min-height:34px}.sp-field b{display:block;font-size:10px}.sp-field span{font-weight:700;font-size:12px;word-break:break-word}.sp-bottom-actions{display:none!important}.sp-search-meta{font-size:11px;margin:5px 0}</style></head><body>'+x.innerHTML+'</body></html>');w.document.close();w.focus();setTimeout(function(){w.print();},400)};
  window.saveSearchProfile=function(){toast('সার্চ প্রোফাইল সেভ করার জন্য নিচের ডাউনলোড ব্যবহার করতে পারেন।')};
  window.editSearchProfile=function(){var x=document.querySelectorAll('#searchProfilePrint .sp-field span');x.forEach(function(s){s.contentEditable=true;s.style.outline='1px dashed #d40000'});toast('✏️ সার্চ প্রোফাইল এডিট মোড চালু হয়েছে')};
  window.downloadSearchProfile=function(){var x=document.getElementById('searchProfilePrint');if(!x)return;var blob=new Blob(['<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Search Profile</title></head><body>'+x.outerHTML+'</body></html>'],{type:'text/html;charset=utf-8'});var u=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u;a.download='Search_Profile.html';document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(function(){URL.revokeObjectURL(u)},1000);toast('সার্চ প্রোফাইল ডাউনলোড হয়েছে')};
  window.doSearch=function(where){var sig=$(where==='login'?'loginSignal':'appSignal').value,q=$(where==='login'?'loginSearch':'appSearch').value.trim();if(!sig)return toast('প্রথমে সংকেত নির্বাচন করুন।');if(!q)return toast('ID / মোবাইল / ইমেইল দিন।');call('searchAll',token,sig,q).then(function(r){if(!r.ok)return toast(r.message);renderSearchProfiles(r.matches||[],r.signal||sig,r.query||q);openWindow('searchWin')}).catch(function(){toast('সার্চ সার্ভার ত্রুটি')})};
})();
</script>

<script id="login-working-safety">
(function(){
  function bindLogin(){
    var btn=document.getElementById('mainLoginBtn');
    var u=document.getElementById('username');
    var p=document.getElementById('password');
    if(!btn||!u||!p||btn.dataset.bound==='1')return;
    btn.dataset.bound='1';
    btn.addEventListener('click',function(ev){ev.preventDefault();if(!btn.disabled)window.login();});
    [u,p].forEach(function(el){el.addEventListener('keydown',function(ev){if(ev.key==='Enter'){ev.preventDefault();login();}});});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',bindLogin);else bindLogin();
  setTimeout(bindLogin,300); setTimeout(bindLogin,1000);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-background-photo-slider-js">
(function(){
  var defaults=[
    {src:'https://makkah-madinah.accor.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/06/Destination_Mecca_17.jpg',label:'মক্কা — মসজিদুল হারাম'},
    {src:'https://musingsofamusafir.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2014/12/12552701_435594356646002_9107830321788115915_n.jpg',label:'মদিনা — মসজিদে নববী'},
    {src:'https://cdn.getyourguide.com/image/format%3Dauto%2Cfit%3Dcrop%2Cgravity%3Dauto%2Cquality%3D60%2Cwidth%3D400%2Cheight%3D265%2Cdpr%3D2/tour_img/63d6d4c5b31ae.jpeg',label:'আল-আকসা'},
    {src:'https://img.southasiajournal.net/assets/img/202603/1773899214_df219dcb8fc4b3328408.jpg',label:'বাংলাদেশের জাতীয় পতাকা'}
  ];
  var userPhotos=[];
  var idx=0,timer=null;
  function render(){
    var box=document.getElementById('headerBackgroundSlides');
    if(!box)return;
    box.innerHTML='';
    var all=defaults.concat(userPhotos);
    all.forEach(function(item,i){
      var img=document.createElement('img');
      img.className='header-background-slide'+(i===0?' active':'');
      img.src=item.src; img.alt=item.label||'Header background'; img.loading='eager';
      box.appendChild(img);
    });
    var del=document.createElement('button');
    del.type='button';
    del.id='headerSlideDeleteBtn';
    del.className='header-slide-delete-btn';
    del.title='বর্তমান Header স্লাইড ডিলেট করুন';
    del.textContent='🗑 ডিলেট';
    del.onclick=function(){ window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide(); };
    box.appendChild(del);
    var overlay=document.createElement('div');
    overlay.className='header-background-overlay';
    box.appendChild(overlay);
    idx=0;
    clearInterval(timer);
    if(all.length>1){
      timer=setInterval(function(){
        var imgs=box.querySelectorAll('.header-background-slide');
        if(!imgs.length)return;
        imgs[idx].classList.remove('active');
        idx=(idx+1)%imgs.length;
        imgs[idx].classList.add('active');
      },6000);
    }
  }
  window.loadHeaderPhotos=function(files){
    if(!files||!files.length)return;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(files,function(file){
      if(!file.type||file.type.indexOf('image/')!==0)return;
      var url=URL.createObjectURL(file);
      userPhotos.push({src:url,label:file.name});
    });
    render();
  };
  /* তালিকা থেকে নির্দিষ্ট স্লাইড বাছাই করার জন্য index bridge */
  window.__setHeaderSlideIndex=function(n){
    var all=defaults.concat(userPhotos);
    n=Number(n);
    if(!isFinite(n)||n<0||n>=all.length)return false;
    idx=n;
    var box=document.getElementById('headerBackgroundSlides');
    if(box){
      var imgs=box.querySelectorAll('.header-background-slide');
      imgs.forEach(function(img,j){img.classList.toggle('active',j===idx);});
    }
    return true;
  };
  window.__getHeaderSlideList=function(){
    return defaults.concat(userPhotos).map(function(x,i){
      return {src:x.src,label:x.label||('Header Slide '+(i+1)),index:i};
    });
  };
  window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide=function(){
    var all=defaults.concat(userPhotos);
    if(!all.length)return;
    var isUser=idx>=defaults.length;
    if(isUser){
      var ui=idx-defaults.length;
      if(ui>=0&&ui<userPhotos.length)userPhotos.splice(ui,1);
    }else{
      if(idx>=0&&idx<defaults.length)defaults.splice(idx,1);
    }
    idx=0;
    render();
    try{
      if(typeof window.toast==='function')window.toast('Header-এর নির্বাচিত স্লাইড ডিলেট করা হয়েছে।');
    }catch(e){}
  };
  function init(){render();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);else init();
})();
</script>


<script>
/* ===== DASHBOARD SLIDER CONTROLS ===== */
var dashboardMediaPlaying=false;
var dashboardMediaTimer=null;
function toggleMediaPlay(){
  if(!media||!media.length)return;
  var el=document.querySelector('#mediaStage video, #mediaStage audio');
  if(el){
    if(el.paused){el.play();dashboardMediaPlaying=true;}else{el.pause();dashboardMediaPlaying=false;}
  }else{
    dashboardMediaPlaying=!dashboardMediaPlaying;
    clearInterval(dashboardMediaTimer);
    if(dashboardMediaPlaying){dashboardMediaTimer=setInterval(function(){nextMedia();},5000);}
  }
  var b=document.getElementById('mediaPlayBtn');
  if(b)b.textContent=dashboardMediaPlaying?'⏸ Pause':'▶ Play';
}
function renderMediaWithPlayState(){
  if(typeof renderMedia==='function')renderMedia();
  var b=document.getElementById('mediaPlayBtn');
  if(b)b.textContent=dashboardMediaPlaying?'⏸ Pause':'▶ Play';
}
</script>

<script id="account-approval-manager-js">
(function(){
const ACCOUNT_PERMS=[['student','স্টুডেন্ট'],['teacher','শিক্ষক'],['donor','দাতা'],['admission','অনলাইন ভর্তি'],['payment','পেমেন্ট'],['finance','আয়/ব্যয়'],['result','রেজাল্ট'],['exam','পরীক্ষার্থী'],['attendance','হাজিরা'],['notice','নোটিশ'],['gallery','গ্যালারি'],['files','ফাইলস'],['sms','SMS'],['print','প্রিন্ট'],['settings','সেটিংস'],['admin','নতুন Admin']];
function superUser(){var u=window.currentUser||currentUser||{};var r=String(u.Role||u.role||'').toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g,'_');return r==='SUPER_ADMIN'||r==='SUPERADMIN'}
window.openAccountManager=async function(){if(!superUser())return toast('শুধু Super Admin এই অংশ ব্যবহার করতে পারবেন।');openWindow('accountManagerWin');await refreshAccountManager()};
window.refreshAccountManager=async function(){
  try{
    var r=await call('getAccountControlData',token);
    if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.message)||'Account তালিকা পাওয়া যায়নি।');
    var body=$('accountManagerWin').querySelector('.win-body');
    body.innerHTML='';
    var wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='account-manager-wrap';
    wrap.innerHTML='<div class="account-manager-title">🔐 Account Approval & Permission Control</div><div class="account-manager-note">এখান থেকে Super Admin নতুন Admin/Madrasa Account অনুমোদন করবেন, কোন কোন কাজ করতে পারবে তা টিক দেবেন, প্রয়োজনে Suspend/Active করবেন এবং Password Reset করতে পারবেন।</div>';
    var tabs=document.createElement('div');tabs.className='account-tabs';
    [['admin','Admin Account'],['madrasa','Madrasa Account'],['all','সব Account']].forEach(function(t){var b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='account-tab';b.textContent=t[1];b.onclick=function(){renderAccountList(t[0])};tabs.appendChild(b)});
    wrap.appendChild(tabs);var list=document.createElement('div');list.id='accountListUi';list.className='account-list';wrap.appendChild(list);body.appendChild(wrap);
    window._accountData=r;renderAccountList('all');
  }catch(e){toast('Account Manager সার্ভার ত্রুটি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e))}
};
window.renderAccountList=function(type){
  var d=window._accountData||{},arr=[];
  if(type==='admin'||type==='all')arr=arr.concat((d.admins||[]).map(function(x){return Object.assign({_type:'admin'},x)}));
  if(type==='madrasa'||type==='all')arr=arr.concat((d.madrasas||[]).map(function(x){return Object.assign({_type:'madrasa'},x)}));
  var box=$('accountListUi');if(!box)return;
  box.innerHTML='';
  if(!arr.length){box.innerHTML='<div class="account-card">কোনো Account পাওয়া যায়নি।</div>';return}
  arr.forEach(function(x){
    var id=x._type==='admin'?x.AdminID:x.MadrasaID;
    var name=x._type==='admin'?(x.NameBN||x.Name||'Admin'):(x.Name||x.NameBN||'Madrasa');
    var status=x.Status||'PENDING';
    var checked=String(x.Permissions||'').split(',').map(function(s){return s.trim()}).filter(Boolean);
    var card=document.createElement('div');card.className='account-card '+(String(status).toUpperCase()==='PENDING'?'pending':'');card.dataset.type=x._type;card.dataset.id=String(id||'');
    var head=document.createElement('div');head.className='account-card-head';head.innerHTML='<span>'+escA(name)+'</span><span>Status: <b>'+escA(status)+'</b></span>';card.appendChild(head);
    var meta=document.createElement('div');meta.className='account-meta';
    [['ID',id],['Username',x.Username||''],['Password',x.Password||''],['Mobile',x.Mobile||''],['Type',_typeLabel(x._type)],['Approved By',x.ApprovedBy||'']].forEach(function(a){var q=document.createElement('div');q.innerHTML=escA(a[0])+': <b>'+escA(a[1])+'</b>';meta.appendChild(q)});card.appendChild(meta);
    var label=document.createElement('div');label.innerHTML='<b>অনুমোদিত কাজ নির্বাচন করুন:</b>';card.appendChild(label);
    var pg=document.createElement('div');pg.className='perm-grid-ui';
    ACCOUNT_PERMS.forEach(function(p){var l=document.createElement('label');var cb=document.createElement('input');cb.className='perm-ui';cb.type='checkbox';cb.dataset.key=p[0];cb.checked=checked.indexOf(p[0])>=0;l.appendChild(cb);l.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '+p[1]));pg.appendChild(l)});card.appendChild(pg);
    var acts=document.createElement('div');acts.className='account-actions-ui';
    var ap=document.createElement('button');ap.type='button';ap.className='approve';ap.textContent='✅ অনুমোদন + Permission Save';ap.onclick=function(){saveAccountApproval(ap)};acts.appendChild(ap);
    var st=document.createElement('button');st.type='button';st.className='stop';st.textContent='⛔ Suspend';st.onclick=function(){changeAccountStatus(st,'SUSPENDED')};acts.appendChild(st);
    var ac=document.createElement('button');ac.type='button';ac.className='active';ac.textContent='▶ Active';ac.onclick=function(){changeAccountStatus(ac,'ACTIVE')};acts.appendChild(ac);
    var rp=document.createElement('button');rp.type='button';rp.className='reset';rp.textContent='🔑 Password Reset';rp.onclick=function(){resetAccountPasswordUi(rp)};acts.appendChild(rp);
    card.appendChild(acts);box.appendChild(card);
  });
};
window.saveAccountApproval=async function(btn){
  var c=btn&&btn.closest?btn.closest('.account-card'):null;if(!c)return toast('Account card পাওয়া যায়নি।');
  var type=c.dataset.type||'',id=c.dataset.id||'';var permissions=[...c.querySelectorAll('.perm-ui:checked')].map(function(x){return x.dataset.key});
  if(!permissions.length&&!confirm('কোনো Permission নির্বাচন করা হয়নি। তবুও Account অনুমোদন করবেন?'))return;
  var old=btn.textContent;btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='⏳ সংরক্ষণ হচ্ছে...';
  try{var r=await call('approveAccountDirect',token,type,id,'ACTIVE',permissions);if(r&&r.ok){c.classList.add('approval-success');toast(r.message||'✅ অনুমোদন সফল হয়েছে।');btn.textContent='✅ অনুমোদন সম্পন্ন';setTimeout(function(){refreshAccountManager()},600)}else{toast((r&&r.message)||'Approval ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।');btn.textContent=old}}catch(e){toast('অনুমোদন ব্যর্থ: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e));btn.textContent=old}finally{setTimeout(function(){btn.disabled=false},800)}
};
window.changeAccountStatus=async function(btn,status){var c=btn.closest('.account-card');if(!c)return;if(!confirm(status==='SUSPENDED'?'এই Account স্থগিত করবেন?':'এই Account সক্রিয় করবেন?'))return;try{var r=await call('setAccountStatus',token,c.dataset.type,c.dataset.id,status);toast((r&&r.message)||'Status পরিবর্তন হয়েছে');if(r&&r.ok)refreshAccountManager()}catch(e){toast('Status পরিবর্তন ব্যর্থ: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e))}};
window.resetAccountPasswordUi=async function(btn){var c=btn.closest('.account-card');if(!c)return;var p=prompt('নতুন Password দিন (কমপক্ষে 4 অক্ষর):','');if(!p)return;try{var r=await call('resetAccountPassword',token,c.dataset.type,c.dataset.id,p);toast((r&&r.message)||'Password reset হয়েছে');if(r&&r.ok)refreshAccountManager()}catch(e){toast('Password reset ব্যর্থ: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e))}};
function _typeLabel(t){return t==='admin'?'Admin Account':'Madrasa Account'}
function escA(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]})}
})();
</script>

<script id="user-request-window-drag-all-2026-09-17">
(function(){
  function initAllWindowDrag(){
    if(typeof dragWindow!=='function')return;
    document.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(function(w){
      if(!w.dataset.dragBound){
        try{dragWindow(w);w.dataset.dragBound='1';}catch(e){}
      }
    });
  }
  function initLater(){
    initAllWindowDrag();
    setTimeout(initAllWindowDrag,300);
    setTimeout(initAllWindowDrag,800);
    setTimeout(initAllWindowDrag,1500);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',initLater);
  else initLater();

  /* Dynamically opened windows also become draggable without changing their
     existing open/close/minimize handlers. */
  new MutationObserver(function(){initAllWindowDrag()})
    .observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="header-running-news-final-js">
(function(){
  function makeHeaderNewsRun(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.header-news').forEach(function(el){
      if(el.querySelector('.running-news-text')) return;
      var text=el.textContent.trim();
      el.textContent='';
      var span=document.createElement('span');
      span.className='running-news-text';
      span.textContent=text;
      el.appendChild(span);
    });
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',makeHeaderNewsRun);
  else makeHeaderNewsRun();
  setTimeout(makeHeaderNewsRun,500);
})();
</script>

<script id="final-user-fixes-js">
(function(){
  var LOC=[];var fallback=[{division:'ঢাকা',district:'ঢাকা',upazila:'কেরাণীগঞ্জ',union:'',ward:'',post:'',village:'কোনাখোলা'}];
  function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');}
  function vals(rows,key){var a=[];rows.forEach(function(r){var v=String(r[key]||'').trim();if(v&&a.indexOf(v)<0)a.push(v)});return a.sort(function(a,b){return a.localeCompare(b,'bn')});}
  function gv(p,k){var x=document.getElementById(p+k+'Select');return x?x.value:'';}
  function setv(p,k,list){var x=document.getElementById(p+k+'Select');if(!x)return;x.innerHTML='<option value="">নির্বাচন করুন</option>'+list.map(function(v){return '<option value="'+esc(v)+'">'+esc(v)+'</option>'}).join('');}
  function rows(p){var d=gv(p,'Division'),di=gv(p,'District'),u=gv(p,'Upazila'),un=gv(p,'Union');return (LOC.length?LOC:fallback).filter(function(r){return(!d||r.division===d)&&(!di||r.district===di)&&(!u||r.upazila===u)&&(!un||r.union===un)})}
  function cascade(p,l){
    var all=LOC.length?LOC:fallback;
    var d=gv(p,'Division'),di=gv(p,'District'),u=gv(p,'Upazila');
    if(l==='Division'){
      var a=all.filter(function(r){return !d||r.division===d;});
      setv(p,'District',vals(a,'district'));['Upazila','Union','Post','Ward','Village'].forEach(function(k){setv(p,k,[]);});
    }else if(l==='District'){
      var b=all.filter(function(r){return (!d||r.division===d)&&(!di||r.district===di);});
      setv(p,'Upazila',vals(b,'upazila'));['Union','Post','Ward','Village'].forEach(function(k){setv(p,k,[]);});
    }else if(l==='Upazila'){
      var c=all.filter(function(r){return (!d||r.division===d)&&(!di||r.district===di)&&(!u||r.upazila===u);});
      setv(p,'Union',vals(c,'union'));setv(p,'Post',vals(c,'post'));setv(p,'Ward',vals(c,'ward'));setv(p,'Village',vals(c,'village'));
    }else if(l==='Union'){
      var e=rows(p);setv(p,'Post',vals(e,'post'));setv(p,'Ward',vals(e,'ward'));setv(p,'Village',vals(e,'village'));
    }
  }
  function bind(p){
    var union=document.getElementById(p+'UnionSelect'),ward=document.getElementById(p+'WardSelect');
    if(union&&!document.getElementById(p+'PostSelect')){var lab=document.createElement('label');lab.innerHTML='ডাকঘর<select id="'+p+'PostSelect" class="field"></select><input id="'+p+'PostManual" class="field location-manual" placeholder="হাতে লিখুন">';if(ward&&ward.closest('label'))ward.closest('label').before(lab);}
    ['Division','District','Upazila','Union'].forEach(function(l){var x=document.getElementById(p+l+'Select');if(x&&!x.dataset.locBound){x.dataset.locBound='1';x.addEventListener('change',function(){cascade(p,l)})}});
    setv(p,'Division',vals(LOC.length?LOC:fallback,'division'));['District','Upazila','Union','Post','Ward','Village'].forEach(function(k){setv(p,k,[])});
  }
  async function loadLoc(){try{var r=await call('getLocations');LOC=Array.isArray(r)&&r.length?r:fallback}catch(e){LOC=fallback}}
  function bindAll(){['f','ad','r'].forEach(function(p){if(document.getElementById(p+'DivisionSelect'))bind(p)})}
  function removeOldTicker(){var r=document.querySelectorAll('.header > .header-running-row');if(r.length>1)r[0].style.display='none';}

  /* Only admission gets applicant/guardian/principal signatures. */
  var baseModuleForm=window.moduleForm;
  window.moduleForm=function(k){
    var html=baseModuleForm(k);
    if(k==='exam'){
      html='<div class="form-grid"><label>Student ID<input id="rStudent" class="field"></label><label>নাম<input id="rName" class="field"></label><label>পরীক্ষা<input id="rExam" class="field"></label>'+
      '<label>বিভাগ<select id="rDivisionSelect" class="field"></select><input id="rDivisionManual" class="field location-manual" placeholder="হাতে লিখুন"></label>'+
      '<label>জেলা<select id="rDistrictSelect" class="field"></select><input id="rDistrictManual" class="field location-manual" placeholder="হাতে লিখুন"></label>'+
      '<label>উপজেলা / থানা<select id="rUpazilaSelect" class="field"></select><input id="rUpazilaManual" class="field location-manual" placeholder="হাতে লিখুন"></label>'+
      '<label>ইউনিয়ন<select id="rUnionSelect" class="field"></select><input id="rUnionManual" class="field location-manual" placeholder="হাতে লিখুন"></label>'+
      '<label>ডাকঘর<select id="rPostSelect" class="field"></select><input id="rPostManual" class="field location-manual" placeholder="হাতে লিখুন"></label>'+
      '<label>ওয়ার্ড<select id="rWardSelect" class="field"></select><input id="rWardManual" class="field location-manual" placeholder="হাতে লিখুন"></label>'+
      '<label>গ্রাম<select id="rVillageSelect" class="field"></select><input id="rVillageManual" class="field location-manual" placeholder="হাতে লিখুন"></label>'+
      '<label class="full">Subject/Marks JSON<textarea id="rSubjects" class="field"></textarea></label></div><button class="primary" onclick="saveResult(\'exam\')">Save</button>';
    }
    return html;
  };
  window.openModule=function(key){
    var labels=Object.fromEntries((modules||[]).map(function(x){return[x[0],x[1]]}));$('winTitle').textContent=labels[key]||key;
    var sig=key==='admission'?'<div class="form-signatures"><div class="signature-box"><div>আবেদনকারীর স্বাক্ষর</div><div class="signature-line"></div></div><div class="signature-box"><div>অভিভাবকের স্বাক্ষর</div><div class="signature-line"></div></div><div class="signature-box"><div>প্রধান শিক্ষিকার স্বাক্ষর</div><div class="signature-line"></div></div><div class="signature-box"><div>প্রিন্সিপাল/মুহতামিম এর স্বাক্ষর</div><div class="signature-line"></div></div></div>':'';
    $('winBody').innerHTML='<div class="form-language-fixed"><span>বাংলা</span><span>العربية</span><span>English</span></div>'+moduleForm(key)+sig+'<div class="form-action-bar"><button type="button" class="primary" onclick="saveCurrentForm()">💾 সেভ</button><button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="printCurrentForm()">🖨️ প্রিন্ট</button><button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="enableFormEdit()">✏️ এডিট</button><button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="downloadCurrentForm()">⬇️ ডাউনলোড</button><button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="clearFormWindow()">🧹 ক্লিয়ার</button></div>';
    $('formWin').classList.remove('minimized');$('formWin').style.height='';openWindow('formWin');setTimeout(bindAll,30);
  };
  var oldGL=window.getLocationValue;window.getLocationValue=function(id){var m=document.getElementById(id+'Manual'),s=document.getElementById(id+'Select');return(m&&m.value.trim())||(s&&s.value)||''};
  var oldSaveResult=window.saveResult;window.saveResult=async function(k){if(k!=='exam')return oldSaveResult(k);var d={StudentID:$('rStudent').value,Name:$('rName').value,Class:'',Exam:$('rExam').value,SubjectData:$('rSubjects').value,Published:'NO',Date:new Date().toISOString(),Division:getLocationValue('rDivision'),District:getLocationValue('rDistrict'),Upazila:getLocationValue('rUpazila'),Union:getLocationValue('rUnion'),Post:getLocationValue('rPost'),Ward:getLocationValue('rWard'),Village:getLocationValue('rVillage')};var r=await call('saveRecord',token,'EXAM_REG',d);toast(r.ok?'পরীক্ষার্থী নিবন্ধন সফলভাবে সংরক্ষণ হয়েছে':(r.message||'ব্যর্থ'))};
  async function init(){removeOldTicker();await loadLoc();bindAll();setTimeout(removeOldTicker,300)}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);else init();
})();
</script>

<script id="safe-location-allsearch-fix-js-2026-09-18">
(function(){
  var LOC=[];
  var FALLBACK=[
    {division:'ঢাকা',district:'ঢাকা',upazila:'কেরাণীগঞ্জ',union:'শুভাঢ্যা',post:'কেরাণীগঞ্জ',ward:'১',village:'কোনাখোলা'},
    {division:'চট্টগ্রাম',district:'চট্টগ্রাম',upazila:'পটিয়া',union:'পটিয়া',post:'পটিয়া',ward:'১',village:'পটিয়া'},
    {division:'রাজশাহী',district:'রাজশাহী',upazila:'পবা',union:'হড়গ্রাম',post:'রাজশাহী',ward:'১',village:'হড়গ্রাম'},
    {division:'খুলনা',district:'খুলনা',upazila:'দৌলতপুর',union:'আড়ংঘাটা',post:'খুলনা',ward:'১',village:'আড়ংঘাটা'},
    {division:'বরিশাল',district:'বরিশাল',upazila:'বরিশাল সদর',union:'রায়পাশা-কড়াপুর',post:'বরিশাল',ward:'১',village:'রায়পাশা'},
    {division:'সিলেট',district:'সিলেট',upazila:'সিলেট সদর',union:'জালালাবাদ',post:'সিলেট',ward:'১',village:'জালালাবাদ'},
    {division:'রংপুর',district:'রংপুর',upazila:'রংপুর সদর',union:'হরিদেবপুর',post:'রংপুর',ward:'১',village:'হরিদেবপুর'},
    {division:'ময়মনসিংহ',district:'ময়মনসিংহ',upazila:'ময়মনসিংহ সদর',union:'খাগডহর',post:'ময়মনসিংহ',ward:'১',village:'খাগডহর'}
  ];
  function el(p,k){return document.getElementById(p+k+'Select')}
  function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}
  function uniq(a){var o=[];(a||[]).forEach(function(v){v=String(v||'').trim();if(v&&o.indexOf(v)<0)o.push(v)});return o}
  function rows(){return LOC.length?LOC:FALLBACK}
  function setList(p,k,list){var x=el(p,k);if(!x)return;var old=x.value;var arr=uniq(list);x.innerHTML='<option value="">'+({'Division':'বিভাগ','District':'জেলা','Upazila':'উপজেলা / থানা','Union':'ইউনিয়ন','Post':'ডাকঘর','Ward':'ওয়ার্ড','Village':'গ্রাম'}[k]||k)+' নির্বাচন করুন</option>'+arr.map(function(v){return '<option value="'+esc(v)+'">'+esc(v)+'</option>'}).join('');if(arr.indexOf(old)>=0)x.value=old}
  function filtered(p){var d=(el(p,'Division')||{}).value||'',di=(el(p,'District')||{}).value||'',u=(el(p,'Upazila')||{}).value||'',un=(el(p,'Union')||{}).value||'';return rows().filter(function(r){return(!d||r.division===d)&&(!di||r.district===di)&&(!u||r.upazila===u)&&(!un||r.union===un)})}
  function cascade(p,k){var all=rows(),d=(el(p,'Division')||{}).value||'',di=(el(p,'District')||{}).value||'',u=(el(p,'Upazila')||{}).value||'';
    if(k==='Division'){var a=all.filter(function(r){return !d||r.division===d});setList(p,'District',a.map(function(r){return r.district}));['Upazila','Union','Post','Ward','Village'].forEach(function(x){setList(p,x,[])})}
    else if(k==='District'){var b=all.filter(function(r){return(!d||r.division===d)&&(!di||r.district===di)});setList(p,'Upazila',b.map(function(r){return r.upazila}));['Union','Post','Ward','Village'].forEach(function(x){setList(p,x,[])})}
    else if(k==='Upazila'){var c=all.filter(function(r){return(!d||r.division===d)&&(!di||r.district===di)&&(!u||r.upazila===u)});['Union','Post','Ward','Village'].forEach(function(x){setList(p,x,c.map(function(r){return r[x.toLowerCase()]}) )})}
    else if(k==='Union'){var e=filtered(p);['Post','Ward','Village'].forEach(function(x){setList(p,x,e.map(function(r){return r[x.toLowerCase()]}) )})}
  }
  function ensurePost(p){var u=el(p,'Union'),w=el(p,'Ward');if(u&&!el(p,'Post')){var lab=document.createElement('label');lab.innerHTML='ডাকঘর<select id="'+p+'PostSelect" class="field"></select><input id="'+p+'PostManual" class="field location-manual" placeholder="হাতে লিখুন / অন্যটি">';if(w&&w.closest('label'))w.closest('label').before(lab)}}
  function bind(p){ensurePost(p);['Division','District','Upazila','Union'].forEach(function(k){var x=el(p,k);if(x&&!x.dataset.safeLocBound){x.dataset.safeLocBound='1';x.addEventListener('change',function(){cascade(p,k)})}});setList(p,'Division',rows().map(function(r){return r.division}));}
  function bindAll(){['f','ad','r'].forEach(function(p){if(el(p,'Division'))bind(p)})}
  function addAllSearch(){var row=document.querySelector('.header-search-row-final');if(!row)return;var old=row.querySelector('.all-search-label-final');if(old)old.remove();if(!row.querySelector('.header-home-final')){var x=document.createElement('button');x.className='primary header-home-final';x.type='button';x.title='Home';x.textContent='🏠 হোম';x.onclick=function(){goHome()};row.insertBefore(x,row.firstChild)}row.style.display='flex';row.style.visibility='visible';row.style.opacity='1'}
  async function loadLoc(){try{var r=await call('getLocations');LOC=Array.isArray(r)?r:(r&&Array.isArray(r.data)?r.data:[])}catch(e){LOC=[]}addAllSearch();bindAll()}
  function start(){addAllSearch();loadLoc();bindAll()}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start);else start();
  setTimeout(addAllSearch,500);setTimeout(bindAll,700);
})();
</script>

<script id="requested-final-restore-2026-09-20-js">
(function(){

<script id="universal-window-player-3d-2026-09-20-js">
(function(){
 var oldDrag=window.dragWindow;
 function makeDrag(w){
   if(!w)return;
   var head=w.querySelector('.win-head');if(!head||head.dataset.universalDrag==='1')return;
   head.dataset.universalDrag='1';
   head.onmousedown=function(e){
     if(e.target.closest('button,input,select,textarea,a'))return;
     e.preventDefault();
     var r=w.getBoundingClientRect(),sx=e.clientX,sy=e.clientY;
     w.style.transform='none';w.style.left=r.left+'px';w.style.top=r.top+'px';
     function move(ev){
       var x=r.left+ev.clientX-sx,y=r.top+ev.clientY-sy;
       var maxX=Math.max(0,window.innerWidth-w.offsetWidth),maxY=window.innerHeight*2;
       w.style.setProperty('left',Math.max(0,Math.min(maxX,x))+'px','important');w.style.setProperty('top',Math.max(70,y)+'px','important');
     }
     function up(){document.removeEventListener('mousemove',move);document.removeEventListener('mouseup',up)}
     document.addEventListener('mousemove',move);document.addEventListener('mouseup',up);
   };
 }

 /* Replace the old media rendering visually without changing the Drive-backed storage. */

   var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');if(!box)return;
   if(!window.media||!media.length){box.innerHTML='<span class="media-center-badge">মিডিয়া আপলোড করে স্লাইড দেখুন</span>';return}
   var i=((window.mediaIndex||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length,x=media[i],u=String(x.DataURL||'');

   if(x.Type==='image')box.innerHTML=badge+'<img src="'+esc(u)+'" alt="">';
   else if(x.Type==='video')box.innerHTML=badge+'<video src="'+esc(u)+'" controls preload="metadata"></video>';
   else box.innerHTML=badge+'<audio src="'+esc(u)+'" controls preload="metadata"></audio>';
   box.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<button class="media-arrow-new left" type="button" onclick="previousMedia()" aria-label="Previous">❮</button><button class="media-arrow-new right" type="button" onclick="nextMedia()" aria-label="Next">❯</button>');

     box.parentElement.appendChild(controls);
   }
 };

   else {clearInterval(window._newMediaTimer);window._newMediaTimer=setInterval(function(){nextMedia()},5000);if(b)b.textContent='⏸️ Pause'}
 };

<script id="final-authoritative-window-drag-js">
(function(){
  function install(w){
    if(!w)return;
    var h=w.querySelector('.win-head');
    if(!h)return;
    h.onmousedown=null;
    h.onpointerdown=null;
    h.onmousedown=function(e){
      if(e.button!==0)return;
      if(e.target.closest('button,input,select,textarea,a'))return;
      e.preventDefault();
      var r=w.getBoundingClientRect();
      var sx=e.clientX,sy=e.clientY,ox=r.left,oy=r.top;
      w.style.setProperty('position','fixed','important');
      w.style.setProperty('transform','none','important');
      w.style.setProperty('left',ox+'px','important');
      w.style.setProperty('top',oy+'px','important');
      w.style.setProperty('right','auto','important');
      w.style.setProperty('bottom','auto','important');
      w.style.setProperty('margin','0','important');
      w.style.setProperty('z-index','2147483646','important');
      function move(ev){
        var x=ox+(ev.clientX-sx),y=oy+(ev.clientY-sy);
        var maxX=Math.max(0,window.innerWidth-w.getBoundingClientRect().width);
        x=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxX,x));
        y=Math.max(0,y);
        w.style.setProperty('left',x+'px','important');
        w.style.setProperty('top',y+'px','important');
      }
      function stop(){
        document.removeEventListener('mousemove',move,true);
        document.removeEventListener('mouseup',stop,true);
      }
      document.addEventListener('mousemove',move,true);
      document.addEventListener('mouseup',stop,true);
    };
  }
  function installAll(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(install);
  }
  window.installFinalWindowDrag=installAll;
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',installAll);
  else installAll();
  setTimeout(installAll,100);
  setTimeout(installAll,500);
  setTimeout(installAll,1200);
  setTimeout(installAll,2500);
  new MutationObserver(function(){installAll()}).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="clean-media-player-fix-js-2026-09-20">
(function(){
  function cleanExtraControls(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.media-player-controls').forEach(function(x){x.remove()});
  }
  function renderClean(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    cleanExtraControls();
    if(!window.media||!media.length){
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-center-badge">মিডিয়া আপলোড করে স্লাইড দেখুন</span>';
      var pb=document.getElementById('mediaPlayBtn');if(pb)pb.textContent='▶ Play';
      return;
    }
    mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;
    var x=media[mediaIndex],u=String(x.DataURL||'');
    var badge='<span class="media-center-badge">'+(typeof esc==='function'?esc(x.Name||('Media '+(mediaIndex+1))):'Media')+'</span>';
    if(x.Type==='image')box.innerHTML=badge+'<img src="'+u+'" alt="">';
    else if(x.Type==='video')box.innerHTML=badge+'<video src="'+u+'" controls playsinline preload="metadata"></video>';
    else box.innerHTML=badge+'<audio src="'+u+'" controls preload="metadata"></audio>';
    box.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<button class="media-arrow-new left" type="button" onclick="prevMedia()">❮</button><button class="media-arrow-new right" type="button" onclick="nextMedia()">❯</button>');
    var pb=document.getElementById('mediaPlayBtn');if(pb)pb.textContent='▶ Play';
    if(typeof renderMediaList==='function')renderMediaList();
  }
  window.renderMedia=renderClean;
  window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){
    var el=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video,#mediaStageContent audio');
    var pb=document.getElementById('mediaPlayBtn');
    if(el){
      if(el.paused){
        var p=el.play();
        if(p&&p.then)p.then(function(){if(pb)pb.textContent='⏸ Pause'}).catch(function(){if(pb)pb.textContent='▶ Play';toast('এই মিডিয়া প্লে করা যাচ্ছে না।')});
      }else{el.pause();if(pb)pb.textContent='▶ Play'}
    }else if(window.media&&media.length){
      if(window.dashboardMediaPlaying){
        window.dashboardMediaPlaying=false;clearInterval(window.dashboardMediaTimer);if(pb)pb.textContent='▶ Play';
      }else{
        window.dashboardMediaPlaying=true;clearInterval(window.dashboardMediaTimer);
        window.dashboardMediaTimer=setInterval(function(){nextMedia()},5000);
        if(pb)pb.textContent='⏸ Pause';
      }
    }
  };
  window.nextMedia=function(){if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)+1)%media.length;renderClean()}};
  window.prevMedia=function(){if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)-1+media.length)%media.length;renderClean()}};
  window.deleteCurrentMedia=async function(){
    if(!window.media||!media.length)return toast('ডিলেট করার মিডিয়া নেই।');
    var x=media[mediaIndex];
    var r=await call('deleteMedia',token,x.Serial);
    if(r&&r.ok){media.splice(mediaIndex,1);mediaIndex=media.length?mediaIndex%media.length:0;renderClean();toast('মিডিয়া মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে।')}else toast((r&&r.message)||'ডিলিট ব্যর্থ');
  };
  function start(){cleanExtraControls();if(window.media&&media.length)renderClean()}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start);else start();
  setTimeout(start,500);setTimeout(start,1500);
})();

<script id="final-clean-media-controls-js-2026-09-20">
(function(){
 function ensureList(){
   var controls=document.querySelector('#mediaStage .dashboard-slider-controls');
   if(!controls)return null;
   var list=document.getElementById('mediaDeleteList');
   if(!list){list=document.createElement('div');list.id='mediaDeleteList';controls.parentElement.appendChild(list);}
   return list;
 }
 function clean(){
   var controls=document.querySelector('#mediaStage .dashboard-slider-controls');
   if(controls){
     controls.querySelectorAll('.slider-arrow').forEach(function(b){b.style.display='none';});
     var load=controls.querySelector('[onclick*="openModule"]');
     var play=controls.querySelector('[onclick*="toggleMediaPlay"]');
     var del=controls.querySelector('[onclick*="deleteCurrentMedia"]');
     if(load){load.textContent='⬆️ লোড';load.style.display='inline-flex';}
     if(play){play.textContent='▶️ প্লে';play.style.display='inline-flex';}
     if(del){del.textContent='🗑️ ডিলেট';del.style.display='inline-flex';}
   }
   var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
   if(box){
     box.querySelectorAll('.media-center-badge,.media-arrow-new').forEach(function(e){e.remove();});
   }
   ensureList();
 }
 window.deleteCurrentMedia=async function(){
   var list=ensureList(); if(!list)return;
   /* Always refresh from server first, so media uploaded in Gallery is visible here. */
   try{
     var fresh=await call('listMedia',token);
     if(Array.isArray(fresh))window.media=fresh;
   }catch(e){}
   if(!window.media||!media.length){
     list.innerHTML='<div>ডিলেট করার কোনো মিডিয়া নেই।</div>';list.classList.add('show');return;
   }
   list.innerHTML='<div style="font-weight:900;margin-bottom:6px">মিডিয়া তালিকা — যেটি মুছতে চান তার ডিলেট চাপুন</div>'+
     media.map(function(x,i){
       var n=typeof escapeHtml==='function'?escapeHtml(x.Name||('Media '+(i+1))):String(x.Name||('Media '+(i+1)));
       return '<div class="media-delete-row"><span>'+n+'</span><button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="deleteMediaByIndex('+i+')">🗑️ ডিলেট</button></div>';
     }).join('');
   list.classList.add('show');
   mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;
   renderAuthoritative();
 };
 window.deleteMediaByIndex=async function(i){
   if(!window.media||!media[i])return;
   var x=media[i],r=await call('deleteMedia',token,x.Serial);
   if(r&&r.ok){
     media.splice(i,1);
     if(mediaIndex>=media.length)mediaIndex=Math.max(0,media.length-1);
     renderMedia();clean();
     var list=ensureList();if(list)window.deleteCurrentMedia();
     toast('মিডিয়া মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে।');
   }else toast((r&&r.message)||'ডিলেট ব্যর্থ');
 };
 window.renderMedia=function(){
   var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');if(!box)return;
   if(!window.media||!media.length){box.innerHTML='<span>মিডিয়া লোড করে দেখুন</span>';clean();return;}
   mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;
   var x=media[mediaIndex],u=String(x.DataURL||''),t=String(x.Type||'').toLowerCase();
   if(t==='image')box.innerHTML='<img src="'+u+'" alt="">';
   else if(t==='video'){
     var vid=(u.match(/[?&]id=([^&]+)/)||[])[1]||'',m=u.match(/\/d\/([^/]+)/);if(!vid&&m)vid=m[1];
     box.innerHTML=vid?'<iframe class="drive-media-frame" src="https://drive.google.com/file/d/'+encodeURIComponent(vid)+'/preview" allow="autoplay; fullscreen" allowfullscreen></iframe>':'<span>ভিডিও পাওয়া যায়নি</span>';
   }else{
     var aid=(u.match(/[?&]id=([^&]+)/)||[])[1]||'',am=u.match(/\/d\/([^/]+)/);if(!aid&&am)aid=am[1];
     box.innerHTML=aid?'<iframe class="drive-media-frame" src="https://drive.google.com/file/d/'+encodeURIComponent(aid)+'/preview" allow="autoplay" allowfullscreen></iframe>':'<span>অডিও পাওয়া যায়নি</span>';
   }
   clean();
 };
 window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){
   var el=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video,#mediaStageContent audio');
   if(el){if(el.paused){el.play().catch(function(){toast('এই মিডিয়া চালানো যাচ্ছে না।')});}else el.pause();}
   else if(window.media&&media.length){toast('প্লেয়ার থেকে ▶ চাপুন।');}
 };
 function start(){clean();}
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start);else start();
 setTimeout(start,300);setTimeout(start,1000);
})();
</script>
