<script id="final-media-sequential-load-fix-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  var seqTimer=null, loadBusy=false;

  function list(){
    return Array.isArray(window.media)?window.media:[];
  }

  function normalise(){
    var m=list();
    if(!m.length){window.media=[];return;}
    var n=Number(window.mediaIndex);
    if(!isFinite(n))n=0;
    window.mediaIndex=((n%m.length)+m.length)%m.length;
  }

  function playCurrent(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    var el=box.querySelector('video,audio');
    if(el){
      el.autoplay=true;
      el.playsInline=true;
      el.preload='auto';
      var p=el.play();
      if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(function(){
        try{el.muted=true;var q=el.play();if(q&&q.catch)q.catch(function(){})}catch(e){}
      });
    }
  }

  function scheduleNext(){
    clearTimeout(seqTimer);
    var m=list();
    if(m.length<=1)return;
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    var el=box.querySelector('video,audio');
    if(el){
      el.onended=function(){
        window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%list().length;
        if(typeof window.renderMedia==='function')window.renderMedia();
        setTimeout(function(){playCurrent();scheduleNext();},120);
      };
    }else{
      /* ছবি নির্দিষ্ট সময় পরে পরের মিডিয়ায় যাবে */
      seqTimer=setTimeout(function(){
        window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%list().length;
        if(typeof window.renderMedia==='function')window.renderMedia();
        setTimeout(function(){scheduleNext();},120);
      },6000);
    }
  }

  function renderAndPlay(){
    normalise();
    if(!list().length)return;
    if(typeof window.renderMedia==='function')window.renderMedia();
    setTimeout(function(){playCurrent();scheduleNext();},100);
  }

  async function refresh(){
    if(loadBusy||typeof window.call!=='function'||!window.token)return;
    loadBusy=true;
    try{
      var r=await window.call('listMedia',window.token);
      var m=Array.isArray(r)?r:(r&&Array.isArray(r.media)?r.media:(r&&Array.isArray(r.rows)?r.rows:[]));
      window.media=m||[];
      normalise();
      if(window.media.length)renderAndPlay();
    }catch(e){}
    loadBusy=false;
  }

  /* Login/token হওয়ার পর Delete চাপা ছাড়াই মিডিয়া নিজে লোড হবে */
  function boot(){
    refresh();
    [700,1600,3000].forEach(function(ms){setTimeout(refresh,ms)});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot);else boot();

  /* Load বাটনে চাপলে আগে মিডিয়া refresh হবে; Delete লাগবে না */
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#mediaStage .slider-center-controls .slider-action');
    if(!b)return;
    var title=(b.getAttribute('title')||'').toLowerCase();
    var txt=(b.textContent||'').toLowerCase();
    if(title.indexOf('upload')>=0||txt.indexOf('load')>=0){
      setTimeout(refresh,350);
      setTimeout(refresh,1400);
    }
    if(title.indexOf('delete')>=0||txt.indexOf('delete')>=0){
      setTimeout(refresh,600);
    }
  },true);

  /* renderMedia অন্য স্ক্রিপ্ট থেকে কল হলেও sequential autoplay সচল থাকবে */
  var oldRender=window.renderMedia;
  if(typeof oldRender==='function'){
    window.renderMedia=function(){
      oldRender.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(function(){playCurrent();scheduleNext();},80);
    };
  }
})();
</script>


<style id="prevent-duplicate-media-names-2026-09-23">
/* একই নামে দ্বিতীয়বার মিডিয়া লোড/সেভ হবে না */
</style>
<script id="prevent-duplicate-media-names-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function key(v){return String(v||'').trim().toLowerCase();}
  var oldUpload=window.uploadMedia;
  if(typeof oldUpload!=='function')return;
  window.uploadMedia=async function(){
    var input=document.getElementById('mediaFiles');
    if(!input||!input.files||!input.files.length)return oldUpload.apply(this,arguments);
    var existing=Array.isArray(window.media)?window.media:[];
    var names={};
    existing.forEach(function(x){var n=key(x&&x.Name);if(n)names[n]=true;});
    var files=Array.prototype.slice.call(input.files);
    var dup=files.filter(function(f){return names[key(f.name)];});
    if(dup.length){
      if(typeof window.toast==='function')window.toast('একই নামে ফাইল আগে থেকেই আছে: '+dup.map(function(f){return f.name}).join(', '));
      /* নতুন নামের ফাইলগুলোই শুধু আপলোড হবে */
      var dt=new DataTransfer();
      files.filter(function(f){return !names[key(f.name)];}).forEach(function(f){dt.items.add(f);});
      input.files=dt.files;
      if(!input.files.length)return;
    }
    return oldUpload.apply(this,arguments);
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="header-final-clock-news-calendar-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function pad(n){return String(n).padStart(2,'0');}
  function updateHeaderClock(){
    var now=new Date();
    var clock=document.getElementById('appClock'), date=document.getElementById('appDate');
    if(clock)clock.textContent=pad(now.getHours())+':'+pad(now.getMinutes())+':'+pad(now.getSeconds());
    if(date){
      var days=['রবিবার','সোমবার','মঙ্গলবার','বুধবার','বৃহস্পতিবার','শুক্রবার','শনিবার'];
      date.textContent=pad(now.getDate())+'-'+pad(now.getMonth()+1)+'-'+now.getFullYear()+'  '+days[now.getDay()];
    }
  }
  function setupNews(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#app > header.header .header-news-row-final .header-news').forEach(function(el){
      var txt=(el.textContent||'').trim()||'জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানার ডিজিটাল প্লাটফর্মে আপনাকে স্বাগতম';
      el.innerHTML='';
      var track=document.createElement('span'); track.className='header-news-track';
      for(var i=0;i<2;i++){
        var item=document.createElement('span');
        item.className='header-news-item';
        item.textContent=txt;
        track.appendChild(item);
      }
      el.appendChild(track);
    });
  }
  function setupCalendar(){
    var s=document.getElementById('headerCalendarSelect');
    if(!s||s.dataset.bound)return;
    s.dataset.bound='1';
    s.onchange=function(){
      var v=s.value;
      if(!v)return;
      var d=new Date(), out='';
      if(v==='en')out='English Calendar: '+d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB');
      if(v==='ar')out='التقويم العربي: '+d.toLocaleDateString('ar-SA-u-ca-islamic');
      if(v==='bn')out='বাংলা ক্যালেন্ডার: '+d.toLocaleDateString('bn-BD-u-ca-beng');
      if(typeof window.toast==='function')window.toast(out);
      s.value='';
    };
  }
  function init(){updateHeaderClock();setupNews();setupCalendar();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);else init();
  setInterval(updateHeaderClock,1000);
  setTimeout(init,700);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-slide-delete-list-final-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function getData(){
    if(typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function'){
      try{return window.__getHeaderSlideList();}catch(e){}
    }
    var box=document.getElementById('headerBackgroundSlides');
    var imgs=box?box.querySelectorAll('.header-background-slide'):[];
    return Array.prototype.map.call(imgs,function(img,i){
      return {src:img.src,label:img.alt||('Header Slide '+(i+1)),index:i};
    });
  }
  function ensureWindow(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteWindow');
    if(w)return w;
    w=document.createElement('div');
    w.id='headerSlideDeleteWindow';
    w.innerHTML='<div class="hsdw-head"><span>Header ছবির তালিকা — যেটি চান ডিলেট করুন</span><button type="button" class="hsdw-close" onclick="closeHeaderSlideDeleteList()">✕</button></div><div class="hsdw-body" id="headerSlideDeleteList"></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(w);
    return w;
  }
  function renderList(){
    var w=ensureWindow(), list=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteList');
    var data=getData();
    list.innerHTML='';
    if(!data.length){
      list.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;font-weight:900;padding:25px">কোনো Header স্লাইড নেই</div>';
      return;
    }
    data.forEach(function(x){
      var row=document.createElement('div');
      row.className='hsdw-item';
      row.innerHTML='<img src="'+x.src.replace(/"/g,'&quot;')+'" alt=""><div class="hsdw-name">'+String(x.label).replace(/[<>&"]/g,function(s){return {'<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','&':'&amp;','"':'&quot;'}[s]})+'</div><button type="button" class="hsdw-del">🗑 ডিলেট</button>';
      row.querySelector('.hsdw-del').onclick=function(){
        window.deleteHeaderSlideByListIndex(x.index);
      };
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    w.classList.add('show');
  }
  window.deleteHeaderSlideByListIndex=function(i){
    /* মূল slider-এর render/arrays-এর ভেতরের delete function ব্যবহার */
    if(typeof window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide!=='function')return;
    var all=window.__headerSlideDeleteState;
    if(!all||!Array.isArray(all))return;
    if(i<0||i>=all.length)return;
    /* বর্তমান slider index-এ নিয়ে গিয়ে authoritative delete চালানো */
    try{
      if(typeof window.__setHeaderSlideIndex==='function')window.__setHeaderSlideIndex(i);
      window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide();
      setTimeout(renderList,80);
    }catch(e){}
  };
  window.openHeaderSlideDeleteList=function(){
    renderList();
  };
  window.closeHeaderSlideDeleteList=function(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteWindow');
    if(w)w.classList.remove('show');
  };
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#headerSlideDeleteBtn');
    if(!b)return;
    e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    renderList();
  },true);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-slide-delete-state-bridge-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  /* Final bridge: use the original slider's private index setter/delete function.
     Do not override either function; the original slider already exposes both correctly. */
  function sync(){
    try{
      var data=typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function'?window.__getHeaderSlideList():[];
      window.__headerSlideDeleteState=data;
    }catch(e){window.__headerSlideDeleteState=[];}
  }
  sync();
  setInterval(sync,500);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-calendar-full-month-final-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  var state={cal:'en',cursor:new Date()};
  var cfg={
    en:{locale:'en-GB',label:'ইংরেজী ক্যালেন্ডার',week:['Sun','Mon','Tue','Wed','Thu','Fri','Sat']},
    ar:{locale:'ar-SA-u-ca-islamic',label:'আরবী ক্যালেন্ডার',week:['الأحد','الإثنين','الثلاثاء','الأربعاء','الخميس','الجمعة','السبت']},
    bn:{locale:'bn-BD-u-ca-beng',label:'বাংলা ক্যালেন্ডার',week:['রবি','সোম','মঙ্গল','বুধ','বৃহস্পতি','শুক্র','শনি']}
  };
  function parts(d,locale){
    var a=new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale,{year:'numeric',month:'numeric',day:'numeric'}).formatToParts(d),o={};
    a.forEach(function(x){if(x.type!=='literal')o[x.type]=x.value});
    return o;
  }
  function monthKey(d,locale){
    var p=parts(d,locale);return p.year+'|'+p.month;
  }
  function monthLabel(d,locale){
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale,{year:'numeric',month:'long'}).format(d);
  }
  function makeDays(){
    var locale=cfg[state.cal].locale, cur=state.cursor;
    var target=monthKey(cur,locale), arr=[], start=new Date(cur.getTime());
    start.setDate(start.getDate()-45);
    for(var i=0;i<140;i++){
      var d=new Date(start.getTime());d.setDate(start.getDate()+i);
      if(monthKey(d,locale)===target)arr.push(d);
    }
    return arr;
  }
  function render(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerCalendarWindow');if(!w)return;
    var c=cfg[state.cal], days=makeDays(), grid=w.querySelector('.hc-grid'), label=w.querySelector('.hc-month-label');
    label.textContent=c.label+' — '+monthLabel(state.cursor,c.locale);
    grid.innerHTML='';
    c.week.forEach(function(x){
      var h=document.createElement('div');h.className='hc-weekday';h.textContent=x;grid.appendChild(h);
    });
    var first=days[0], lead=first?first.getDay():0;
    for(var z=0;z<lead;z++){var e=document.createElement('div');e.className='hc-day empty';grid.appendChild(e);}
    var today=new Date(), todayKey=monthKey(today,c.locale)+'|'+parts(today,c.locale).day;
    days.forEach(function(d){
      var p=parts(d,c.locale), cell=document.createElement('div');cell.className='hc-day';
      if(monthKey(d,c.locale)+'|'+p.day===todayKey)cell.classList.add('today');
      cell.innerHTML='<div><div class="num">'+p.day+'</div><span class="greg">'+d.getDate()+'-'+(d.getMonth()+1)+'</span></div>';
      grid.appendChild(cell);
    });
  }
  function ensure(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerCalendarWindow');
    if(w)return w;
    w=document.createElement('div');w.id='headerCalendarWindow';
    w.innerHTML='<div class="hc-head"><button class="hc-nav" id="hcPrev">‹</button><div class="hc-title">ক্যালেন্ডার</div><button class="hc-nav" id="hcNext">›</button><button class="hc-close" onclick="closeHeaderCalendar()">✕</button></div><div class="hc-body"><div class="hc-month-label"></div><div class="hc-grid"></div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(w);
    w.querySelector('#hcPrev').onclick=function(){state.cursor.setMonth(state.cursor.getMonth()-1);render();};
    w.querySelector('#hcNext').onclick=function(){state.cursor.setMonth(state.cursor.getMonth()+1);render();};
    return w;
  }
  window.openHeaderCalendar=function(cal){
    state.cal=cal||'en';state.cursor=new Date();
    ensure().classList.add('show');render();
  };
  window.closeHeaderCalendar=function(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerCalendarWindow');if(w)w.classList.remove('show');
  };
  var t=setInterval(function(){
    var s=document.getElementById('headerCalendarSelect');
    if(!s)return;
    clearInterval(t);
    s.onchange=function(){
      var v=s.value;if(!v)return;
      openHeaderCalendar(v);
      s.value='';
    };
  },100);
})();
</script>

<script id="search-profile-photo-and-actions-authoritative-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]})}
  function photoUrl(v){
    var s=String(v==null?'':v).trim();
    if(!s)return '';
    if(/^data:image\//i.test(s))return s;
    if(/^DRIVE:/i.test(s))return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(s.slice(6).trim())+'&sz=w800';
    var m=s.match(/(?:[?&]id=|\/d\/|\/file\/d\/|open\?id=)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,})/i);
    if(m)return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(m[1])+'&sz=w800';
    if(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}$/.test(s))return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(s)+'&sz=w800';
    if(/^https?:\/\//i.test(s))return s;
    return '';
  }
  window.__searchProfilePhotoUrl=photoUrl;
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#searchProfilePrint .sp-bottom-actions button');
    if(!b)return;
    var t=(b.textContent||'').trim();
    if(t.indexOf('সেভ')>=0 && typeof window.saveSearchProfile==='function'){e.preventDefault();window.saveSearchProfile();return}
    if(t.indexOf('এডিট')>=0 && typeof window.editSearchProfile==='function'){e.preventDefault();window.editSearchProfile();return}
    if(t.indexOf('প্রিন্ট')>=0 && typeof window.printSearchProfile==='function'){e.preventDefault();window.printSearchProfile();return}
    if(t.indexOf('ডাউনলোড')>=0 && typeof window.downloadSearchProfile==='function'){e.preventDefault();window.downloadSearchProfile();return}
  },true);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-delete-open-list-authoritative-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function bind(){
    var b=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteBtn');
    if(!b||b.dataset.finalBound==='1')return;
    b.dataset.finalBound='1';
    b.onclick=function(e){
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      if(typeof window.openHeaderSlideDeleteList==='function')window.openHeaderSlideDeleteList();
    };
  }
  function syncState(){
    try{
      if(typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function'){
        window.__headerSlideDeleteState=window.__getHeaderSlideList();
      }
    }catch(e){}
    bind();
  }
  setTimeout(syncState,100);
  setInterval(syncState,500);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-delete-list-authoritative-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function removeAt(i){
    var list=typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function'?window.__getHeaderSlideList():[];
    i=Number(i);
    if(!isFinite(i)||i<0||i>=list.length)return;
    if(typeof window.__setHeaderSlideIndex==='function')window.__setHeaderSlideIndex(i);
    if(typeof window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide==='function')window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide();
    setTimeout(function(){
      var w=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteWindow');
      var body=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteList');
      if(!w||!body)return;
      body.innerHTML='';
      var now=typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function'?window.__getHeaderSlideList():[];
      if(!now.length){
        body.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;font-weight:900;padding:25px">কোনো Header স্লাইড নেই</div>';
        return;
      }
      now.forEach(function(x){
        var row=document.createElement('div');
        row.className='hsdw-item';
        var img=document.createElement('img');img.src=x.src||'';img.alt='';
        var name=document.createElement('div');name.className='hsdw-name';name.textContent=x.label||('Header Slide '+(x.index+1));
        var btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';btn.className='hsdw-del';btn.textContent='🗑 ডিলেট';
        btn.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();removeAt(x.index);};
        row.appendChild(img);row.appendChild(name);row.appendChild(btn);body.appendChild(row);
      });
      w.classList.add('show');
    },120);
  }
  window.deleteHeaderSlideByListIndex=removeAt;
})();
</script>

<script id="final-search-profile-photo-actions-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function photoCandidates(src){
    var s=String(src||'').trim(), out=[];
    if(!s)return out;
    if(/^data:image\//i.test(s))return [s];
    var m=s.match(/(?:[?&]id=|\/d\/|\/file\/d\/|open\?id=)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,})/i);
    if(!m && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}$/.test(s))m=[s,s];
    if(m){
      var id=m[1]||m[0];
      out.push('https://drive.google.com/uc?export=view&id='+encodeURIComponent(id));
      out.push('https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(id)+'&sz=w1000');
      return out;
    }
    if(/^https?:\/\//i.test(s))out.push(s);
    return out;
  }
  function fixPhotos(root){
    root=root||document;
    root.querySelectorAll('#searchProfilePrint .sp-photo').forEach(function(img){
      if(img.dataset.photoFinal==='1')return;
      var src=img.getAttribute('src')||'';
      var cs=photoCandidates(src);
      if(!cs.length)return;
      img.dataset.photoFinal='1';
      var n=0;
      function next(){
        if(n>=cs.length)return;
        img.src=cs[n++];
      }
      img.onerror=next;
      next();
    });
  }
  function ensureActions(){
    var p=document.getElementById('searchProfilePrint');
    if(!p)return;
    var old=p.querySelector('.sp-bottom-actions');
    if(old)return;
    var d=document.createElement('div');
    d.className='sp-bottom-actions';
    d.innerHTML='<button type="button" class="primary">💾 সেভ</button><button type="button" class="secondary">✏️ এডিট</button><button type="button" class="secondary">🖨️ প্রিন্ট</button><button type="button" class="secondary">⬇️ ডাউনলোড</button>';
    d.children[0].onclick=function(){if(typeof window.saveSearchProfile==='function')window.saveSearchProfile();};
    d.children[1].onclick=function(){if(typeof window.editSearchProfile==='function')window.editSearchProfile();};
    d.children[2].onclick=function(){if(typeof window.printSearchProfile==='function')window.printSearchProfile();};
    d.children[3].onclick=function(){if(typeof window.downloadSearchProfile==='function')window.downloadSearchProfile();};
    p.appendChild(d);
  }
  function run(){ensureActions();fixPhotos(document);}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run);else run();
  new MutationObserver(function(){run();}).observe(document.documentElement,{subtree:true,childList:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="final-header-delete-open-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function openList(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteWindow');
    if(!w){
      w=document.createElement('div');
      w.id='headerSlideDeleteWindow';
      w.innerHTML='<div class="hsdw-head"><span>Header ছবির তালিকা</span><button type="button" class="hsdw-close">✕</button></div><div class="hsdw-body" id="headerSlideDeleteList"></div>';
      document.body.appendChild(w);
      w.querySelector('.hsdw-close').onclick=function(){w.classList.remove('show');};
    }
    var body=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteList');
    var list=[];
    try{if(typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function')list=window.__getHeaderSlideList()||[];}catch(e){}
    body.innerHTML='';
    list.forEach(function(x,i){
      var row=document.createElement('div');row.className='hsdw-item';
      var im=document.createElement('img');im.src=x.src||'';im.alt='';
      var nm=document.createElement('div');nm.className='hsdw-name';nm.textContent=x.label||('Header Slide '+(i+1));
      var del=document.createElement('button');del.type='button';del.className='hsdw-del';del.textContent='🗑 ডিলেট';
      del.onclick=function(e){
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
        try{
          if(typeof window.__setHeaderSlideIndex==='function')window.__setHeaderSlideIndex(x.index);
          if(typeof window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide==='function')window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide();
        }catch(err){}
        setTimeout(openList,150);
      };
      row.appendChild(im);row.appendChild(nm);row.appendChild(del);body.appendChild(row);
    });
    if(!list.length)body.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;font-weight:900;padding:25px">কোনো Header স্লাইড নেই</div>';
    w.classList.add('show');
  }
  function hook(){
    var b=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteBtn');
    if(b)b.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();openList();};
  }
  hook();setInterval(hook,300);
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#headerSlideDeleteBtn');
    if(b){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();openList();}
  },true);
  window.openHeaderSlideDeleteList=openList;
})();
</script>

<script id="search-profile-a4-pad-3col-js-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]})}
  function first(r,keys){for(var i=0;i<keys.length;i++){if(r&&r[keys[i]]!==undefined&&r[keys[i]]!==null&&String(r[keys[i]])!=='')return r[keys[i]]}return ''}
  function qr(text){return 'https://quickchart.io/qr?size=160&text='+encodeURIComponent(text||'Jannatul Baqi Digital Platform 2026')}
  function photo(v){
    var s=String(v||'').trim(); if(!s)return '';
    if(/^data:image\\//i.test(s))return s;
    var m=s.match(/(?:[?&]id=|\\/d\\/|\\/file\\/d\\/|open\\?id=)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,})/i);
    if(/^DRIVE:/i.test(s))return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(s.slice(6).trim())+'&sz=w600';
    if(m)return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(m[1])+'&sz=w600';
    if(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}$/.test(s))return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(s)+'&sz=w600';
    return /^https?:\\/\\//i.test(s)?s:'';
  }
  window.renderSearchProfilesA4Pad=function(rows,signal,q){
    rows=rows||[];

    var cards=rows.map(function(r){
      var title=first(r,['NameBN','নাম','Name','NameEN'])||'স্টুডেন্ট তথ্য';
      var fields=[
        ['স্টুডেন্ট ID',first(r,['StudentID','ID','Serial','Student Id'])],
        ['নাম',first(r,['NameBN','Name','নাম'])],

        ['English Name',first(r,['NameEN'])],

        ['ঠিকানা',first(r,['Address','ঠিকানা'])],
        ['জন্ম নিবন্ধন',first(r,['BirthReg','জন্ম নিবন্ধন'])],
        ['NID',first(r,['NID'])]
      ];
      return '<div class="sp-student"><div class="sp-student-top"><div class="sp-student-info"><div class="sp-student-title">'+esc(title)+' — স্টুডেন্ট তথ্য</div></div></div><div class="sp-data">'+fields.map(function(f){return '<div class="sp-field"><b>'+esc(f[0])+'</b><span>'+esc(f[1])+'</span></div>'}).join('')+'</div></div>';
    }).join('');
    var actions='<div class="sp-bottom-actions"><button class="primary" onclick="saveSearchProfile()">💾 সেভ</button><button class="secondary" onclick="editSearchProfile()">✏️ এডিট</button><button class="secondary" onclick="printSearchProfile()">🖨️ প্রিন্ট</button><button class="secondary" onclick="downloadSearchProfile()">⬇️ ডাউনলোড</button></div>';
    $('searchBody').innerHTML='<div id="searchProfilePrint" class="search-profile"><div class="sp-pad-meta" style="font-size:10px;margin:2px 0 5px;font-weight:800">সার্চ ফলাফল: '+esc(signal)+' / '+esc(q)+' — '+rows.length+' জন</div>'+head+cards+actions+'</div>';

<!-- ===== FINAL SEARCH PROFILE ROUTING FIX — 2026-09-23 ===== -->
<script id="final-search-profile-routing-fix-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  /* সর্বশেষ doSearch/table override যেন নতুন A4 Pad renderer-কে আর bypass না করে। */
  window.doSearch=function(where){
    var sigEl=document.getElementById(where==='login'?'loginSignal':'appSignal');
    var qEl=document.getElementById(where==='login'?'loginSearch':'appSearch');
    var sig=sigEl?sigEl.value:'', q=qEl?(qEl.value||'').trim():'';
    if(!sig)return toast('প্রথমে সংকেত নির্বাচন করুন।');
    if(!q)return toast('ID / মোবাইল / ইমেইল দিন।');
    var searchToken=(where==='login')?'':(window.token||token||'');
    call('searchAll',searchToken,sig,q).then(function(r){
      if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.message)||'সার্চের ফল পাওয়া যায়নি।');
      if(typeof window.renderSearchProfilesA4Pad==='function'){
        window.renderSearchProfilesA4Pad(r.matches||[],r.signal||sig,r.query||q);
      }else if(typeof window.renderSearchProfiles==='function'){
        window.renderSearchProfiles(r.matches||[],r.signal||sig,r.query||q);
      }else{
        var body=document.getElementById('searchBody');
        if(body)body.innerHTML='<h3>'+(r.signal||sig)+' / '+(r.query||q)+' — '+(r.count||0)+' ফলাফল</h3>'+table(r.matches||[]);
      }
      openWindow('searchWin');
    }).catch(function(e){toast('সার্চ সার্ভার ত্রুটি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e));});
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="a4-pad-header-size-logo-fix-js-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  var old=window.renderSearchProfilesA4Pad;if(typeof old!=='function')return;
  window.renderSearchProfilesA4Pad=function(rows,signal,q){
    old(rows,signal,q);var p=document.getElementById('searchProfilePrint');if(!p)return;
    var head=p.querySelector('.sp-pad-head');if(!head)return;
    var qr=head.querySelector('.sp-pad-qr');
    if(qr){var logo=document.createElement('img');logo.className='sp-pad-logo';logo.src=(document.getElementById('loginLogo')&&document.getElementById('loginLogo').src)||'';logo.alt='Institution Logo';qr.replaceWith(logo)}
  };
})();
</script>

#loginPage .loginLogoWrap{text-align:center!important;margin-left:auto!important;margin-right:auto!important}

/* A4 pad-এর header: বামে আসল Login logo, মাঝে প্রতিষ্ঠানের তথ্য, ডানে ছাত্রের ছবি */

<script id="final-a4-pad-logo-center-fix-js-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  var old=window.renderSearchProfilesA4Pad;
  if(typeof old!=='function')return;
  window.renderSearchProfilesA4Pad=function(rows,signal,q){
    old(rows,signal,q);
    var p=document.getElementById('searchProfilePrint');if(!p)return;
    var head=p.querySelector('.sp-pad-head');if(!head)return;
    var qr=head.querySelector('.sp-pad-qr');
    if(qr){
      var src='';
      var loginLogo=document.getElementById('loginLogo');
      if(loginLogo&&loginLogo.src)src=loginLogo.src;
      if(src){
        var logo=document.createElement('img');logo.className='sp-pad-logo';logo.src=src;logo.alt='Institution Logo';
        qr.replaceWith(logo);
      }
    }
  };
})();
</script>


<!-- ===== FINAL DIRECT VIDEO PLAYER + MEDIA LIST + DELETE LIVE FIX — 2026-09-23 ===== -->
<style id="final-direct-video-media-live-fix-2026-09-23">
#mediaStageContent video.media-direct-player{
  width:100%!important;height:100%!important;max-width:none!important;max-height:none!important;
  object-fit:contain!important;display:block!important;background:#000!important;
}
#mediaList{display:block!important;width:100%!important;margin-top:8px!important}
.media-list-item{
  display:flex!important;align-items:center!important;justify-content:space-between!important;
  gap:8px!important;padding:7px 9px!important;margin:4px 0!important;
  border:2px solid var(--border)!important;background:var(--panel)!important;
}
.media-list-item span{min-width:0!important;overflow:hidden!important;text-overflow:ellipsis!important;white-space:nowrap!important}
.media-list-item button{flex:0 0 auto!important}
</style>
<script id="final-direct-video-media-live-fix-js-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  var loading=false, timer=null;

  function arr(){ return Array.isArray(window.media)?window.media:[]; }
  function norm(){
    var m=arr(); if(!m.length){window.media=[];window.mediaIndex=0;return;}
    var n=Number(window.mediaIndex); if(!isFinite(n))n=0;
    window.mediaIndex=((n%m.length)+m.length)%m.length;
  }
  function direct(x){
    return String((x&&(
      x.DirectURL||x.DataURL||x.Url||x.URL||x.FileURL||x.fileUrl
    ))||'').trim();
  }
  function typeOf(x){return String(x&& (x.Type||x.type)||'').toLowerCase();}
  function esc(v){
    return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){
      return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c];
    });
  }

  function draw(){
    norm();
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    var m=arr();
    if(!m.length){
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়া আপলোড করে স্লাইড দেখুন</span>';
      renderList();
      return;
    }
    var x=m[window.mediaIndex]||{}, t=typeOf(x), u=direct(x);
    box.innerHTML='';
    if(t==='video'){
      var v=document.createElement('video');
      v.className='media-direct-player';
      v.controls=true;
      v.playsInline=true;
      v.preload='auto';
      v.muted=true;
      if(u)v.src=u;
      v.addEventListener('ended',function(){
        var a=arr();if(!a.length)return;
        window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%a.length;
        draw();
        autoplay();
      });
      v.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',function(){autoplay();},{once:true});
      v.addEventListener('canplay',function(){autoplay();},{once:true});
      v.addEventListener('error',function(){});
      box.appendChild(v);
      setTimeout(autoplay,60);
    }else if(t==='audio'){
      var a=document.createElement('audio');
      a.controls=true;a.preload='auto';a.src=u;
      a.addEventListener('ended',function(){
        var m2=arr();if(!m2.length)return;
        window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%m2.length;
        draw();autoplay();
      });
      box.appendChild(a);
      setTimeout(autoplay,60);
    }else{
      var im=document.createElement('img');
      im.src=u;im.alt=String(x.Name||'');
      im.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;display:block;background:#111';
      im.addEventListener('error',function(){box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">ফটো চালানো যাচ্ছে না।</span>'});
      box.appendChild(im);
      clearTimeout(timer);
      timer=setTimeout(function(){
        var a=arr();if(a.length<=1)return;
        window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%a.length;
        draw();
      },6000);
    }
    renderList();
  }

  function autoplay(){
    var v=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video.media-direct-player,#mediaStageContent audio');
    if(!v)return;
    try{
      v.muted=true;
      var p=v.play();
      if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(function(){});
    }catch(e){}
  }

  function renderList(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaList');if(!box)return;
    var m=arr();
    box.innerHTML=m.map(function(x,i){
      var active=(i===Number(window.mediaIndex||0))?' style="font-weight:900"':'';
      return '<div class="media-list-item"><span'+active+'>'+esc(x.Name||('Media '+(i+1)))+'</span>'+
        '<button type="button" class="secondary" data-direct-media-delete="'+i+'">🗑 Delete</button></div>';
    }).join('');
  }

  async function refresh(){
    if(loading||typeof window.call!=='function'||!window.token)return;
    loading=true;
    try{
      var r=await window.call('listMedia',window.token);
      var m=Array.isArray(r)?r:(r&&Array.isArray(r.media)?r.media:(r&&Array.isArray(r.rows)?r.rows:[]));
      window.media=m||[];
      norm();
      draw();
    }catch(e){}
    loading=false;
  }

  async function del(i){
    var m=arr(), x=m[i];if(!x)return;
    try{
      var r=await window.call('deleteMedia',window.token,x.Serial);
      if(r&&r.ok){
        m.splice(i,1);
        if(window.mediaIndex>=m.length)window.mediaIndex=Math.max(0,m.length-1);
        draw();
        renderList();
        setTimeout(refresh,100);
        if(typeof window.toast==='function')window.toast('মিডিয়া মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে।');
      }else if(typeof window.toast==='function'){
        window.toast((r&&r.message)||'ডিলিট ব্যর্থ');
      }
    }catch(e){
      if(typeof window.toast==='function')window.toast('ডিলিটে সার্ভার ত্রুটি হয়েছে।');
    }
  }

  window.renderMedia=draw;
  window.renderMediaList=renderList;
  window.deleteMediaByIndex=del;
  window.nextMedia=function(){
    var m=arr();if(!m.length)return;
    window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%m.length;draw();autoplay();
  };
  window.prevMedia=function(){
    var m=arr();if(!m.length)return;
    window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)-1+m.length)%m.length;draw();autoplay();
  };
  window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){autoplay()};

  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-direct-media-delete]');
    if(b){
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      del(Number(b.getAttribute('data-direct-media-delete')));
      return;
    }
  },true);

  function boot(){
    refresh();
    var tries=0;
    clearInterval(window.__mediaDirectLiveTimer);
    window.__mediaDirectLiveTimer=setInterval(function(){
      if(window.token){refresh();tries++;if(tries>=12)clearInterval(window.__mediaDirectLiveTimer);}
    },1000);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot);else boot();

  var oldUpload=window.uploadMedia;
  if(typeof oldUpload==='function'){
    window.uploadMedia=async function(){
      var r=await oldUpload.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(refresh,150);
      setTimeout(refresh,900);
      return r;
    };
  }
})();
</script>
